 121:     @org.springframework.context.event.EventListener(org.springframework.boot.context.event.ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
 122:     public void start() {
 123:         if(fix142Enabled && !source.enabled())throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 live scheduling requires LIVE mode");
 124:         if(admissionCeiling<2 || freshReserve<1 || freshReserve>=admissionCeiling)
 125:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-142 invalid admission ceiling/reserve");
 126:         protocol.ceiling=admissionCeiling;protocol.reserve=freshReserve;
 127:         analysisDispatch.historicalCapacity(historicalThreads);
 128:         analysisDispatch.historicalEnabled(historicalEnabled);
 129:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled(ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled);
 130:         analysisDispatch.ownerTimeoutQuietSeconds(ownerTimeoutReconciliationQuietSeconds);
 131:         // FIX-146: do not throw ApplicationReadyEvent for recoverable symbol state.
 132:         // Every dispatch surface uses this gate; diagnostics and operator APIs stay up.
 133:         initializeTradingReadiness();
 134:         analysisDispatch.acknowledgmentEnabled(acknowledgmentEnabled);
 135:         analysisDispatch.freshPriority(fix142Enabled);
 136:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 137:         if(fix142Enabled) {
 138:             freshClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discoverFresh,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 139:             protectionClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchProtectionTicks,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 140:         }
 141:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileCompletedWorkAcknowledgments,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 142:         if("OFF".equals(source.mode()))return;
 143:         if(source.enabled())readinessClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::refreshTradingReadiness,10,10,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 144:         // Independent clocks: slow source reads or CallerRuns analysis saturation
 145:         // cannot monopolize Spring's default scheduler and stop price dispatch.
 146:         discoveryClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::discover,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 147:         priceClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchPrices,0,50,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 148:         analysisClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchAnalysis,0,100,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 149:         if(historicalEnabled)historyClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::dispatchHistoricalAnalysis,1000,1000,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 150:         log.info("[FIX-140][HISTORY_CONFIGURATION] enabled={}, savedSignalsAndCandlesRetained=true",historicalEnabled);
 151:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::monitor,30,30,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 152:         // FIX-142 review correction: old protection attempts age out of the recent-price
 153:         // selector, so fencing/alerting must not depend on another claim selecting them.
 154:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::monitorProtectionAttempts,5,5,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 155:         // FIX-141 Phase 2: runs independently of claim()'s REVIEW_BATCH_LIMIT check, so a lane
 156:         // that crossed 16 unresolved reviews can still drain over successive runs.
 157:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews,60,60,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 158:         // FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: owner-timeout reconciliation is diagnostic-only
 159:         // (logs candidates, performs no mutation) and off until the team enables it and sets a
 160:         // quiet window (see SharedAnalysisDispatcher#ownerTimeoutReconciliationEnabled/Seconds);
 161:         // scheduling it unconditionally here is safe since it no-ops while disabled, and even
 162:         // enabled it cannot change analysis_status itself.
 163:         monitorClock.scheduleWithFixedDelay(analysisDispatch::reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews,300,300,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 164:         log.info("[FIX-138][ANALYSIS_CONFIGURATION] liveThreads=8, historyThreads={}, independentClocks=true, livePriority=true, historicalWalletExecution=false",historicalThreads);
 165:     }
 166:     public void validateCutover() {
 167:         log.info("[FIX-132][SOURCE_CONFIGURATION] mode={}, readPool=3, priceWorkers=2, pendingPerSymbol=200, publicationSource=collector",source.mode());
 168:         if(!source.enabled())return;
 169:         if(!activationApproved)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 LIVE requires explicit measured cutover acceptance (activation-approved)");
 170:         var unattributed=jdbc.queryForList(OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS);
 171:         if(!unattributed.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][UNATTRIBUTED_WORK] reconcile before LIVE: "+unattributed);
 172:         var invalidOwners=jdbc.queryForList("""
 173:             SELECT w.signal_id,w.status,w.source_event_id FROM signal_processing_work w
 174:             LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
 175:             WHERE w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
 176:             AND (d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code
 177:                  OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time OR d.closed=0
 178:                  OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY')) ORDER BY w.signal_id LIMIT 25
 179:             """);
 180:         if(!invalidOwners.isEmpty())throw new IllegalStateException("[FIX-132][PROCESSING_OWNER_INCONSISTENT] reconcile before LIVE: "+invalidOwners);
 181:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols())validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
 182:     }
 183:     // Shared by strict acceptance checks and per-symbol operational readiness.
 184:     private Map<String,Object> validateSymbolCutover(String symbol) {
 185:             var state=readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 186:             if(state.isEmpty() || !SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state.getFirst()))
 187:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing/unapproved/review-required cutover state for "+symbol);
 188:             var boundary=state.getFirst();
 189:             if(num(boundary,"cutover_sequence")<0 || num(boundary,"discovered_sequence")<num(boundary,"cutover_sequence"))
 190:                 throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 invalid sequence boundary for "+symbol);
 191:             Integer approval=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_approval WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_sequence=? AND cutover_at=? AND approved_by=? AND approved_at=? AND approval_reference=?",Integer.class,
 192:                 symbol,boundary.get("approved_sequence"),boundary.get("approved_cutover_at"),boundary.get("approved_by"),boundary.get("approved_at"),boundary.get("approval_reference"));
 193:             if(approval==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 missing approval audit for "+symbol);
 194:             Long latest=source.feedReader().queryForObject("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",Long.class,symbol);
 195:             if(latest==null || latest<num(state.getFirst(),"discovered_sequence"))throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 source sequence regressed for "+symbol);
 196:             Set<String> required=new HashSet<>(intervals.getIntervals());required.add("1m");
 197:             for(String interval:required) {
 198:                 Long count=source.reader().queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT id FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 LIMIT 300) c",Long.class,symbol,interval);
 199:                 if(count==null || count<300)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 incomplete source history: "+symbol+" "+interval);
 200:             }
 201:             return boundary;
 202:     }
 203: 
 204:     private boolean symbolAllowed(String symbol) {
 205:         return !source.enabled() || (readinessSymbols.contains(symbol) || !tradingReadiness.managed())
 206:             && tradingReadiness.allowed(symbol);
 207:     }
 208:     public Map<String,Object> tradingReadiness() {
 209:         var snapshot=new LinkedHashMap<String,Object>(tradingReadiness.snapshot());
 210:         snapshot.put("configuredSymbols",readinessSymbols);
 211:         snapshot.put("automaticFirstCutoverEnabled",automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
 212:         snapshot.put("waitingCutoverDoesNotAutoApprove",!automaticFirstCutoverEnabled);
 213:         return snapshot;
 214:     }
 215:     void initializeTradingReadiness() {
 216:         if(!source.enabled())return;
 217:         tradingReadiness.begin();
 218:         refreshTradingReadiness();
 219:         log.warn("[FIX-146][STARTUP_READINESS] {}; process stays available, validated first cutovers may activate automatically, uncertain symbols receive no new shared attempts",tradingReadiness.snapshot());
 220:     }
 221:     private static final String SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
 222:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE w.symbol=? AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
 223:     private static final String UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS=OperatorWorkCancellation.UNATTRIBUTED_BLOCKERS
 224:         .replace("WHERE w.source_event_id IS NULL","WHERE (w.symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(w.symbol)='') AND w.source_event_id IS NULL");
 225:     // Dedicated clock and round-robin budget: one slow probe cannot monopolize trading
 226:     // clocks or permanently strand later symbols. JDBC timeouts are not total deadlines.
 227:     synchronized void refreshTradingReadiness() {
 228:         if(stopped || !source.enabled())return;
 229:         List<String> symbols;
 230:         try {symbols=List.copyOf(coins.enabledSymbols());}
 231:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 232:             for(String symbol:readinessSymbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
 233:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_INVENTORY_FAILED]",failure);return;
 234:         }
 235:         readinessSymbols=symbols;tradingReadiness.retainSymbols(symbols);
 236:         boolean unscoped;
 237:         try {unscoped=!readinessJdbc.queryForList(UNSCOPED_BLOCKERS).isEmpty()
 238:                 || !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id IS NOT NULL AND (symbol IS NULL OR TRIM(symbol)='') AND status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED') LIMIT 1").isEmpty();}
 239:         catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 240:             for(String symbol:symbols)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED);
 241:             log.error("[FIX-146][READINESS_SCOPE_CHECK_FAILED]",failure);return;
 242:         }
 243:         long deadline=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(20);
 244:         for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
 245:             if(checked>0 && System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
 246:             String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(readinessCursor++,symbols.size()));
 247:             if(unscoped)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.UNSCOPED_WORK);
 248:             else if(!activationApproved)recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.ACTIVATION_REQUIRED);
 249:             else {
 250:                 try {
 251:                     if(!readinessJdbc.queryForList(SYMBOL_LEGACY_BLOCKERS,symbol).isEmpty()) {
 252:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.LEGACY_WORK_REVIEW);continue;
 253:                     }
 254:                     // Inconsistent cross-symbol ownership blocks both affected symbols.
 255:                     var owners=readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
 256:                         SELECT w.signal_id FROM signal_processing_work w
 257:                         LEFT JOIN shared_market_event_delivery d ON d.source_event_id=w.source_event_id
 258:                         WHERE (w.symbol=? OR d.symbol=?) AND w.source_event_id IS NOT NULL
 259:                           AND w.status NOT IN ('COMPLETED','EXPIRED','LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED')
 260:                           AND (w.status='OPERATOR_CANCELLED' OR d.source_event_id IS NULL OR w.symbol IS NULL
 261:                             OR w.interval_code IS NULL OR w.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.symbol IS NULL
 262:                             OR d.interval_code IS NULL OR d.candle_open_time IS NULL OR d.closed IS NULL
 263:                             OR d.analysis_status IS NULL OR w.symbol<>d.symbol
 264:                             OR w.interval_code<>d.interval_code OR w.candle_open_time<>d.candle_open_time
 265:                             OR d.closed=0 OR d.analysis_status NOT IN ('RUNNING','REVIEW_REQUIRED','EXECUTION_RETRY'))
 266:                         LIMIT 1
 267:                         """,symbol,symbol);
 268:                     if(!owners.isEmpty()) {recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNER_INCONSISTENT);continue;}
 269:                     if(automaticFirstCutoverEnabled)automaticActivation.activateFirst(symbol,intervals.getIntervals());
 270:                     var validated=validateSymbolCutover(symbol);
 271:                     if(!fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("SELECT symbol FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? AND active_delivery_state<>'NONE'",symbol).isEmpty()) {
 272:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.OWNERSHIP_MODE_MISMATCH);continue;
 273:                     }
 274:                     if(fix142Enabled && !readinessJdbc.queryForList("""
 275:                         SELECT d.source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery d WHERE d.symbol=? AND phase BETWEEN 1 AND 3
 276:                         AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shared_market_consumer_state s WHERE s.symbol=d.symbol
 277:                           AND s.active_delivery_event_id=d.source_event_id AND s.active_delivery_state IN ('ACTIVE','REVIEW_REQUIRED')) LIMIT 1
 278:                         """,symbol).isEmpty()) {
 279:                         recordReadiness(symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.PARTIAL_DELIVERY);continue;
 280:                     }
 281:                     // Avoid periodic INSERT/locking for already published history. A changed
 282:                     // boundary is revalidated next pass; dispatch still checks durable approval.
 283:                     Integer history=readinessJdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at=? AND cutover_sequence=?",Integer.class,
 284:                         symbol,validated.get("cutover_at"),validated.get("cutover_sequence"));
 285:                     Boolean stillApproved=history!=null && history>0;
 286:                     if(!stillApproved)stillApproved=readinessTransaction.execute(tx->{
 287:                         var state=readinessJdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",symbol);
 288:                         if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.approved(state))return false;
 289:                         for(String field:List.of("cutover_at","cutover_sequence","approved_sequence","approved_cutover_at","approved_at","approved_by","approval_reference"))
 290:                             if(!Objects.equals(state.get(field),validated.get(field)))return false;
 291:                         readinessJdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO shared_market_cutover_history(symbol,cutover_at,cutover_sequence) VALUES(?,?,?)",
 292:                             symbol,state.get("cutover_at"),state.get("cutover_sequence"));return true;
 293:                     });
 294:                     recordReadiness(symbol,Boolean.TRUE.equals(stillApproved)?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.READY:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED);
 295:                 } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 296:                     recordReadiness(symbol,readinessReason(failure));
 297:                     log.debug("[FIX-146][READINESS_CHECK_FAILED] symbol={}",symbol,failure);
 298:                 }
 299:             }
 300:             if(System.nanoTime()>=deadline)break;
 301:         }
 302:     }
 303:     private SharedTradingReadiness.Reason readinessReason(RuntimeException failure) {
 304:         String message=String.valueOf(failure.getMessage());
 305:         if(message.contains("missing/unapproved/review-required cutover"))return automaticFirstCutoverEnabled
 306:             ?SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.AUTOMATIC_ACTIVATION_WAITING:SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CUTOVER_REQUIRED;
 307:         if(message.contains("missing approval audit"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.APPROVAL_AUDIT_MISSING;
 308:         if(message.contains("invalid sequence boundary"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.INVALID_BOUNDARY;
 309:         if(message.contains("source sequence regressed"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_REGRESSED;
 310:         if(message.contains("incomplete source history"))return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.SOURCE_HISTORY_MISSING;
 311:         return SharedTradingReadiness.Reason.CHECK_FAILED;
 312:     }
 313:     private void recordReadiness(String symbol,SharedTradingReadiness.Reason reason) {
 314:         var before=tradingReadiness.record(symbol,reason);
 315:         if(before==null || before.reason()!=reason) {
 316:             Fix142Metrics.count("READINESS_"+reason.name());
 317:             log.warn("[FIX-146][SYMBOL_READINESS] symbol={}, reason={}, checkedUtc={}, checkedKsa={}; audited first-activation policy, no uncertain retry",
 318:                 symbol,reason,Instant.now(),Instant.now().atOffset(java.time.ZoneOffset.ofHours(3)));
 319:         }
 320:     }
 321: 
 322:     public void discover() {
 323:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
 324:         try {
 325:             for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) {
 326:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))continue;
 327:                 var state=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 328:                 if(state.isEmpty()) {
 329:                     if(source.enabled()) { log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}; explicit cutover boundary missing",symbol); continue; }
 330:                     var seq=source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT last_sequence FROM market_data_stream_cursor WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 331:                     if(seq.isEmpty()) continue;
 332:                     long n=((Number)seq.getFirst().get("last_sequence")).longValue();
 333:                     jdbc.update("INSERT IGNORE INTO shared_market_consumer_state(symbol,cutover_sequence,cutover_at,discovered_sequence,status,cutover_source) VALUES(?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),?,'PENDING_CUTOVER','AUTO_OBSERVED')",symbol,n,n);
 334:                     continue;
 335:                 }
 336:                 if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state.getFirst(),source.enabled())) continue;
 337:                 int pending=fix142Enabled?-1:jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4",Integer.class,symbol);
 338:                 boolean discoveryHeartbeat=System.nanoTime()-discoveryLogTimes.getOrDefault(symbol,0L)>=TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30);
 339:                 if(discoveryHeartbeat)discoveryLogTimes.put(symbol,System.nanoTime());
 340:                 if(!fix142Enabled && pending>=200) {
 341:                     if(discoveryHeartbeat)log.warn("[FIX-132][CONSUMER_LAG] symbol={}, pending={}; discovery paused before analysis admission",symbol,pending);
 342:                     continue;
 343:                 }
 344:                 long after=((Number)state.getFirst().get("discovered_sequence")).longValue();
 345:                 var rows=source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT * FROM market_data_stream_event WHERE symbol=? AND symbol_sequence>? ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT ?",symbol,after,fix142Enabled?32:Math.min(100,200-pending));
 346:                 log.debug("[FIX-137][DISCOVERY_BATCH] symbol={}, afterSequence={}, pending={}, fetched={}",
 347:                     symbol,after,pending,rows.size());
 348:                 if(discoveryHeartbeat)log.info("[FIX-138][DISCOVERY_PROGRESS] symbol={}, afterSequence={}, pendingPricePhases={}, fetched={}, lastFetchedObservationUtc={}",
 349:                     symbol,after,pending,rows.size(),rows.isEmpty()?null:time(rows.getLast(),"observed_at"));
 350:                 // Shared connection is returned before any local transaction starts.
 351:                 transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
 352:                     var locked=lockSymbol(symbol);
 353:                     if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(locked,source.enabled()) || ((Number)locked.get("discovered_sequence")).longValue()!=after) return;
 354:                     long next=after;
 355:                     Map<String,Integer> batchSlots=new HashMap<>();
 356:                     for(var e:rows) {
 357:                         long sequence=num(e,"symbol_sequence");
 358:                         if(sequence!=next+1) {
 359:                             jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET status='SEQUENCE_GAP' WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 360:                             log.error("[FIX-132][SEQUENCE_GAP] symbol={}, expected={}, found={}",symbol,next+1,sequence);break;
 361:                         }
 362:                         if(fix142Enabled) {
 363:                             String interval=str(e,"interval_code");
 364:                             int available=batchSlots.computeIfAbsent(interval,key->Math.max(0,protocol.ceiling-protocol.reserve-protocol.exposure(symbol,key,Instant.now())));
 365:                             var admission=protocol.admit(e,symbol,available>0);
 366:                             if(admission==Fix142DeliveryProtocol.AdmissionResult.NO_CAPACITY)break;
 367:                             if(admission==Fix142DeliveryProtocol.AdmissionResult.INSERTED)batchSlots.put(interval,available-1); // duplicates validated; checkpoint remains contiguous
 368:                         } else {
 369:                         jdbc.update("""
 370:                             INSERT INTO shared_market_event_delivery(source_event_id,symbol,symbol_sequence,interval_code,
 371:                             candle_open_time,candle_close_time,closed,observed_at,received_at,source_created_at,price,source,classification)
 372:                             VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
 373:                             """,e.get("id"),symbol,sequence,e.get("interval_code"),e.get("candle_open_time"),e.get("candle_close_time"),
 374:                             e.get("closed"),e.get("observed_at"),e.get("received_at"),e.get("created_at"),e.get("price"),e.get("source"),e.get("classification"));
 375:                         }
 376:                         next=sequence;
 377:                     }
 378:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET discovered_sequence=? WHERE symbol=?",next,symbol);
 379:                 });
 380:             }
 381:             sourceStatus("AVAILABLE",null);
 382:         } catch(Exception e) {
 383:             try { sourceStatus("SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE",shortError(e)); } catch(Exception diagnostic) { log.error("[FIX-132][HEALTH_RECORD_FAILED]",diagnostic); }
 384:             long now=System.nanoTime();
 385:             if(now-lastSourceLog>TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5)) {
 386:                 lastSourceLog=now;log.error("[FIX-132][SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE] discovery failed; checkpoint not silently advanced",e);
 387:             }
 388:         }
 389:     }
 390:     public void dispatchPrices() {
 391:         if(stopped || "OFF".equals(source.mode())) return;
 392:         try {
 393:             // At most TWO submitted tasks, so newFixedThreadPool's internal queue cannot grow.
 394:             List<String> symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();
 395:             for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
 396:                 if(activePrices.size()>=2) break;
 397:                 String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(nextSymbol++,symbols.size()));
 398:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !activePrices.add(symbol))continue;
 399:                 try { prices.execute(()->{try {
 400:                     int count=fix142Enabled?jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4 LIMIT 8",symbol).size():jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4",Integer.class,symbol);
 401:                     for(int n=0;n<Math.min(8,count)&&!stopped;n++) processNext(symbol);
 402:                 } finally { activePrices.remove(symbol); }}); }
 403:                 catch(RuntimeException rejected) { activePrices.remove(symbol); throw rejected; }
 404:             }
 405:         } catch(Exception e) { log.error("[FIX-132][DISPATCH_FAILED]",e); }
 406:     }
 407:     private Map<String,Object> lockSymbol(String symbol) {
 408:         return jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=? FOR UPDATE",symbol);
 409:     }
 410:     private boolean beginProtection(String symbol,long id,String attempt) {
 411:         return Boolean.TRUE.equals(transaction.execute(tx->{
 412:             var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 413:             if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled()))return false;
 414:             var event=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 415:             if(num(event,"phase")!=1 || !"1m".equals(event.get("interval_code")))return true;
 416:             if(event.get("protection_started_at")==null) {
 417:                 // Durable START precedes business. A process crash cannot turn an
 418:                 // already-started wallet attempt into a fresh automatic retry.
 419:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_started_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_owner=?,status='PROTECTION_RUNNING' WHERE source_event_id=?",attempt,id);
 420:                 return true;
 421:             }
 422:             if(attempt.equals(event.get("protection_owner")))return true;
 423:             // Never steal/re-execute a started attempt. After 30s, fence it into
 424:             // review; a worker already in its transaction holds this row through
 425:             // commit, while one resuming later must observe REVIEW_REQUIRED.
 426:             if(time(event,"protection_started_at").plusSeconds(30).isBefore(Instant.now())) {
 427:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error='Started protection outcome requires reconciliation; no crash retry' WHERE source_event_id=?",id);
 428:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 429:                 log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}, event={}; previous protection attempt unconfirmed",symbol,id);
 430:             }
 431:             return false;
 432:         }));
 433:     }
 434:     void processNext(String symbol) {
 435:         if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))return;
 436:         Long id=null;
 437:         try(var timing=com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource.context("symbol="+symbol+" phase=SELECT")) {
 438:             var candidates=fix142Enabled ? transaction.execute(tx->{
 439:                 var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 440:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled()))return List.<Map<String,Object>>of();
 441:                 var selected=protocol.select(symbol,state);
 442:                 return selected==null?List.<Map<String,Object>>of():List.of(selected);
 443:             }) : jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND phase<4 ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT 1",symbol);
 444:             if(candidates.isEmpty())return;
 445:             id=num(candidates.getFirst(),"source_event_id");
 446:             log.debug("[FIX-137][DELIVERY_START] event={}, symbol={}",id,symbol);
 447:             final long eventId=id;
 448:             final String attemptToken=fix142Enabled?jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT active_delivery_owner_token FROM shared_market_consumer_state WHERE symbol=?",String.class,symbol):UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 449:             // Small bounded turn: one event, four independently committed phases.
 450:             for(int stage=0;stage<4;stage++) {
 451:                 final int expected=stage;
 452:                 if(source.enabled() && expected==1) {
 453:                     if(fix142Enabled) {
 454:                         ProtectionClaim result=claimProtectionAttempt(symbol,eventId,attemptToken);
 455:                         if(result==ProtectionClaim.BLOCKED || result==ProtectionClaim.REVIEW)return;
 456:                         if(result==ProtectionClaim.CLAIMED && !prepareProtectionApply(symbol,eventId,attemptToken))return;
 457:                     } else if(!beginProtection(symbol,eventId,attemptToken))return;
 458:                 }
 459:                 int attempts=0;
 460:                 while(true) {
 461:                 try(var phaseTiming=com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource.context("symbol="+symbol+" event="+eventId+" phase="+expected);var phaseSpan=Fix142Metrics.phase(symbol,eventId,expected)) {
 462:                 transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
 463:                     var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 464:                     if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled())) {
 465:                         if(fix142Enabled&&expected==1)restoreUnenteredClaim(eventId,attemptToken);
 466:                         return;
 467:                     }
 468:                     if(fix142Enabled && !protocol.owned(state,eventId,attemptToken)) {
 469:                         if(expected==1)restoreUnenteredClaim(eventId,attemptToken);
 470:                         return;
 471:                     }
 472:                     var e=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",eventId);
 473:                     if(num(e,"phase")!=expected || "REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(e.get("status"))) {
 474:                         if(fix142Enabled&&expected==1)restoreUnenteredClaim(eventId,attemptToken);
 475:                         return;
 476:                     }
 477:                     if(source.enabled() && expected==1 && "1m".equals(e.get("interval_code"))
 478:                             && e.get("protection_completed_at")==null && !attemptToken.equals(e.get("protection_owner")))return;
 479:                     if(!source.enabled()) {
 480:                         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET phase=4,status='OBSERVED',analysis_status='OBSERVED' WHERE source_event_id=?",eventId);return;
 481:                     }
 482:                     boolean postCutover=num(e,"symbol_sequence")>num(state,"cutover_sequence")
 483:                         && time(e,"observed_at")!=null && time(e,"observed_at").isAfter(time(state,"cutover_at"));
 484:                     boolean canonical="1m".equals(e.get("interval_code"));
 485:                     String reason = protectionReason(symbol,state,e,Instant.now());
 486:                     boolean eligible="ELIGIBLE".equals(reason);
 487:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET owner_token=?,status='PROCESSING' WHERE source_event_id=?",owner,eventId);
 488:                     if(expected==0 && canonical && e.get("observed_at")!=null) {
 489:                         jdbc.update("""
 490:                             INSERT IGNORE INTO market_price_event(symbol,observed_at,price,source,source_event_id,source_sequence,source_received_at,delivery_status)
 491:                             VALUES(?,?,?,'COLLECTOR_KLINE_LIVE',?,?,?,'PENDING')
 492:                             """,symbol,e.get("observed_at"),e.get("price"),eventId,e.get("symbol_sequence"),e.get("received_at"));
 493:                     }
 494:                     if(expected==1 && (!fix142Enabled || e.get("protection_completed_at")==null)) {
 495:                         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET eligibility_reason=? WHERE source_event_id=?",reason,eventId);
 496:                     }
 497:                     if(expected==1 && canonical) {
 498:                         if(fix142Enabled && e.get("protection_completed_at")!=null) {
 499:                             Fix142Metrics.count("SEQUENTIAL_PROTECTION_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
 500:                             String outcome=str(e,"protection_outcome");
 501:                             if(!Set.of("APPLIED","REJECTED").contains(outcome))throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 legacy protection outcome requires review");
 502:                             jdbc.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=? WHERE source_event_id=?","APPLIED".equals(outcome)?"APPLIED":"HISTORICAL_ONLY",eventId);
 503:                         } else {
 504:                             // FIX-150: entry intent committed before any in-process/DB effect.
 505:                             if(fix142Enabled && !"APPLYING".equals(e.get("protection_apply_state")))return;
 506:                             if(fix142Enabled && earlierProtection(symbol,num(e,"symbol_sequence"),Instant.now())) {
 507:                                 // A late earlier admission can arrive AFTER entry-intent commit.
 508:                                 // This return precedes onPrice, so restoring CLAIMED is proven safe.
 509:                                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_apply_state='CLAIMED' WHERE source_event_id=? AND protection_owner=? AND protection_apply_state='APPLYING'",eventId,attemptToken);
 510:                                 return;
 511:                             }
 512:                             if(eligible) {
 513:                                 protection.onPrice(symbol,(java.math.BigDecimal)e.get("price"));
 514:                                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET last_observed_at=?,last_price=? WHERE symbol=?",e.get("observed_at"),e.get("price"),symbol);
 515:                             }
 516:                             jdbc.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=? WHERE source_event_id=?",eligible?"APPLIED":"HISTORICAL_ONLY",eventId);
 517:                             if(fix142Enabled)jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_outcome=?,protection_apply_state='COMPLETED' WHERE source_event_id=?",eligible?"APPLIED":"REJECTED",eventId);
 518:                             else jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE source_event_id=?",eventId);
 519:                         }
 520:                     }
 521:                     if(expected==2 && canonical) {
 522:                         String applied=jdbc.query("SELECT delivery_status FROM market_price_event WHERE source_event_id=?",r->r.next()?r.getString(1):"UNKNOWN",eventId);
 523:                         if("APPLIED".equals(applied)) observer.onPrice(symbol,(java.math.BigDecimal)e.get("price"),Instant.now());
 524:                         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET observer_status=? WHERE source_event_id=?","APPLIED".equals(applied)?"COMPLETED":"NOT_APPLICABLE",eventId);
 525:                     }
 526:                     if(expected==3) {
 527:                         boolean live=postCutover && !time(e,"observed_at").isAfter(Instant.now())
 528:                             && time(e,"candle_close_time").isAfter(time(state,"cutover_at"))
 529:                             && "LIVE".equals(e.get("classification")) && "LIVE_WEBSOCKET".equals(e.get("source"));
 530:                         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status=?,analysis_status=? WHERE source_event_id=?",
 531:                             live?"COMPLETED":"HISTORICAL_ONLY",truth(e.get("closed"))?"PENDING":"NOT_APPLICABLE",eventId);
 532:                         if(!live && truth(e.get("closed"))) {
 533:                             Instant afterGrace=time(e,"candle_close_time").plusSeconds(SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(str(e,"interval_code"))).plusNanos(1_000_000);
 534:                             jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET analysis_status='HISTORICAL_DEFERRED',analysis_not_before=? WHERE source_event_id=?",Timestamp.from(afterGrace),eventId);
 535:                             log.debug("[FIX-132][HISTORICAL_DEFERRED] event={}, until={}",eventId,afterGrace);
 536:                         }
 537:                     }
 538:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET phase=? WHERE source_event_id=?",expected+1,eventId);
 539:                     if(fix142Enabled)protocol.progress(symbol,eventId,attemptToken,expected+1);
 540:                 });
 541:                 phaseSpan.returned();
 542:                 log.debug("[FIX-137][DELIVERY_STAGE_RETURNED] event={}, symbol={}, expectedPhase={}; transaction returned, may have been a guarded no-op",eventId,symbol,expected);
 543:                 break;
 544:                 } catch(com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock initial) {
 545:                     // FIX-124's one safe retry is retained AFTER rollback. This marker
 546:                     // is emitted only before position evaluation/wallet effects begin.
 547:                     if(expected!=1 || ++attempts>=2)throw initial;
 548:                     log.warn("[FIX-132][INITIAL_LOCK_RETRY] symbol={}, event={}",symbol,eventId);
 549:                 } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 550:                     if(expected!=2)throw failure;
 551:                     // FIX-124 observer failures never prevented future protection.
 552:                     // Record uncertainty without retrying diagnostic side effects.
 553:                     transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{lockSymbol(symbol);
 554:                         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET phase=3,observer_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error=? WHERE source_event_id=? AND phase=2",shortError(failure),eventId);
 555:                         if(fix142Enabled)protocol.progress(symbol,eventId,attemptToken,3);
 556:                     });
 557:                     log.warn("[FIX-132][OBSERVER_REVIEW_REQUIRED] event={}; protection retained, no observer retry",eventId,failure);
 558:                     break;
 559:                 }
 560:                 }
 561:             }
 562:         } catch(Exception failure) {
 563:             if(id!=null) {
 564:                 final long failedId=id;
 565:                 // Unknown commits are reconciled manually. Never overwrite a committed
 566:                 // phase marker or replay a potentially committed wallet side effect.
 567:                 try { transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{lockSymbol(symbol);
 568:                     if(fix142Enabled)jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET active_delivery_state='REVIEW_REQUIRED' WHERE symbol=? AND active_delivery_event_id=?",symbol,failedId);
 569:                     else jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' WHERE symbol=?",symbol);
 570:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error=? WHERE source_event_id=?",shortError(failure),failedId);
 571:                 }); } catch(Exception recording) { log.error("[FIX-132][FAILURE_RECORD_FAILED] symbol={}",symbol,recording); }
 572:             }
 573:             log.error("[FIX-132][REVIEW_REQUIRED] symbol={}, event={}; no automatic wallet retry",symbol,id,failure);
 574:         }
 575:     }
 576:     // FIX-142: independent source reads, whole-batch reservations under the same symbol lock.
 577:     public void discoverFresh() {
 578:         if(stopped || !fix142Enabled || !source.enabled())return;
 579:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) {
 580:         if(!symbolAllowed(symbol))continue;
 581:         try(var timing=com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource.context("symbol="+symbol+" phase=FRESH_ADMISSION")) {
 582:             Instant now=Instant.now();List<Map<String,Object>> found=new ArrayList<>();
 583:             String columns="id,symbol,symbol_sequence,interval_code,candle_open_time,candle_close_time,closed,observed_at,received_at,created_at,price,source,classification";
 584:             found.addAll(source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT "+columns+" FROM market_data_stream_event WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND source='LIVE_WEBSOCKET' AND classification='LIVE' AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT 32",symbol,Timestamp.from(now.minusSeconds(15)),Timestamp.from(now)));
 585:             for(String interval:List.of("1m","5m","15m","1h","4h","1d"))
 586:                 found.addAll(source.feedReader().queryForList("SELECT "+columns+" FROM market_data_stream_event WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND source='LIVE_WEBSOCKET' AND classification='LIVE' AND candle_close_time BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY candle_close_time DESC,symbol_sequence LIMIT 2",symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(now.minusSeconds(SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(interval))),Timestamp.from(now)));
 587:             transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
 588:                 var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 589:                 if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,true))return;
 590:                 Map<String,Integer> slots=new HashMap<>();Set<Long> seen=new HashSet<>();int inserted=0;
 591:                 for(var e:found) {
 592:                     if(!seen.add(num(e,"id")))continue;
 593:                     if(num(e,"symbol_sequence")<=num(state,"cutover_sequence") || time(e,"observed_at")==null || !time(e,"observed_at").isAfter(time(state,"cutover_at")))continue;
 594:                     String interval=str(e,"interval_code");
 595:                     int available=slots.computeIfAbsent(interval,key->Math.max(0,protocol.ceiling-protocol.exposure(symbol,key,Instant.now())));
 596:                     var admission=protocol.admit(e,symbol,available>0);
 597:                     if(admission==Fix142DeliveryProtocol.AdmissionResult.INSERTED){slots.put(interval,available-1);inserted++;}
 598:                 }
 599:                 for(int i=0;i<inserted;i++)Fix142Metrics.count("FRESH_ADMITTED_COMMIT_PENDING");
 600:                 log.debug("[FIX-142][FRESH_ADMISSION] symbol={}, inserted={}, contiguousCheckpointUnchanged=true",symbol,inserted);
 601:             });
 602:         }catch(Exception failure){log.error("[FIX-142][FRESH_ADMISSION_FAILED] symbol={}",symbol,failure);}
 603:         }
 604:     }
 605:     private String protectionReason(String symbol,Map<String,Object> state,Map<String,Object> e,Instant now) {
 606:         if(!symbol.equals(e.get("symbol")))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Protection identity mismatch");
 607:         if(!"1m".equals(e.get("interval_code")))return "NON_CANONICAL_INTERVAL";
 608:         if(num(e,"symbol_sequence")<=num(state,"cutover_sequence")||time(e,"observed_at")==null
 609:             || !time(e,"observed_at").isAfter(time(state,"cutover_at")))return "PRE_CUTOVER_OR_UNKNOWN_TIME";
 610:         if(((java.math.BigDecimal)e.get("price")).signum()<=0)return "NON_POSITIVE_PRICE";
 611:         return SharedEventPolicy.priceReason(str(e,"source"),str(e,"classification"),time(e,"observed_at"),time(state,"last_observed_at"),now);
 612:     }
 613:     enum ProtectionClaim { CLAIMED, COMPLETED, NOT_APPLICABLE, BLOCKED, REVIEW }
 614:     private boolean earlierProtection(String symbol,long sequence,Instant now) {
 615:         // Split the OR: old never-started backlog must not force a chronological scan
 616:         // before the recent window. Started uncertainty is retained at any age.
 617:         if(!jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND protection_completed_at IS NULL AND protection_started_at IS NOT NULL AND symbol_sequence<? LIMIT 1",symbol,sequence).isEmpty())return true;
 618:         return !jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND protection_completed_at IS NULL AND protection_started_at IS NULL AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND symbol_sequence<? LIMIT 1",symbol,Timestamp.from(now.minusSeconds(15)),Timestamp.from(now),sequence).isEmpty();
 619:     }
 620:     ProtectionClaim claimProtectionAttempt(String symbol,long id,String attempt) {
 621:         return transaction.execute(tx->{
 622:             var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 623:             if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,source.enabled()))return ProtectionClaim.BLOCKED;
 624:             var e=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 625:             if(!symbol.equals(e.get("symbol")))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-142 protection symbol mismatch");
 626:             if(!"1m".equals(e.get("interval_code")))return ProtectionClaim.NOT_APPLICABLE;
 627:             if(e.get("protection_completed_at")!=null)return ProtectionClaim.COMPLETED;
 628:             if("REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(e.get("status")))return ProtectionClaim.REVIEW;
 629:             if(e.get("protection_started_at")!=null) {
 630:                 if(attempt.equals(e.get("protection_owner")))return ProtectionClaim.CLAIMED;
 631:                 if(time(e,"protection_started_at").plusSeconds(30).isBefore(Instant.now())) {
 632:                     // FIX-150: a later timeout must never erase the initiating exception.
 633:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error=COALESCE(last_error,'FIX-142 unresolved protection; no automatic retry') WHERE source_event_id=?",id);
 634:                     return ProtectionClaim.REVIEW;
 635:                 }
 636:                 return ProtectionClaim.BLOCKED;
 637:             }
 638:             if(earlierProtection(symbol,num(e,"symbol_sequence"),Instant.now()))return ProtectionClaim.BLOCKED;
 639:             // FIX-142 review correction: an earlier-only barrier does not bound claims arriving
 640:             // out of sequence. Permit two outstanding attempts, explicitly counted under this lock.
 641:             // Saturation defers new attempts; it NEVER steals or resets an uncertain attempt.
 642:             Integer outstanding=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND protection_started_at IS NOT NULL AND protection_completed_at IS NULL",Integer.class,symbol);
 643:             if(outstanding>=2){Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_OVERLOAD");log.error("[FIX-142][PROTECTION_OVERLOAD] symbol={}, outstanding={}; no claim evicted",symbol,outstanding);return ProtectionClaim.BLOCKED;}
 644:             jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_started_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_owner=?,status='PROTECTION_RUNNING',protection_apply_state='CLAIMED' WHERE source_event_id=?",attempt,id);
 645:             return ProtectionClaim.CLAIMED;
 646:         });
 647:     }
 648:     /** FIX-150: durable entry intent is a separate, non-nested transaction. CLAIMED
 649:      * can therefore be retired as never entered, even after a crash. APPLYING is
 650:      * uncertainty unless completion committed; token equality alone never permits retry. */
 651:     boolean prepareProtectionApply(String symbol,long id,String token) {
 652:         return Boolean.TRUE.equals(transaction.execute(tx->{
 653:             var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 654:             if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,true))return false;
 655:             var e=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 656:             if(!symbol.equals(e.get("symbol"))||!"1m".equals(e.get("interval_code")))return false;
 657:             // FIX-153 validation found the pre-existing FIX-152 test failure: stale tokens
 658:             // must not receive a success acknowledgment merely because an attempt is terminal.
 659:             if(!token.equals(e.get("protection_owner")))return false;
 660:             if(e.get("protection_completed_at")!=null)return true;
 661:             if("REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(e.get("status")))return false;
 662:             if(!"CLAIMED".equals(e.get("protection_apply_state")))return false;
 663:             if(earlierProtection(symbol,num(e,"symbol_sequence"),Instant.now()))return false;
 664:             if(!time(e,"observed_at").isAfter(Instant.now().minusSeconds(15))) {
 665:                 // Leave CLAIMED for the atomic retirement+marker+audit transaction.
 666:                 // Separately completing it here could strand a marker after a crash.
 667:                 return false;
 668:             }
 669:             int changed=jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_apply_state='APPLYING' WHERE source_event_id=? AND protection_owner=? AND protection_apply_state='CLAIMED' AND protection_completed_at IS NULL",id,token);
 670:             if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-150 protection entry conflict");
 671:             return true;
 672:         }));
 673:     }
 674:     public void dispatchProtectionTicks() {
 675:         if(stopped || !fix142Enabled || !source.enabled())return;
 676:         var symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();
 677:         for(int checked=0;checked<symbols.size();checked++) {
 678:             // FIX-146: readiness deadline belongs only to its dedicated probe loop.
 679:             String symbol=symbols.get(Math.floorMod(nextProtectionSymbol++,symbols.size()));
 680:             if(activeProtection.size()>=2)break;
 681:             if(!symbolAllowed(symbol) || !activeProtection.add(symbol))continue;
 682:             try{protectionPool.execute(()->{
 683:                 try(var timing=com.crypto.infrastructure.diagnostics.JdbcTimingDataSource.context("symbol="+symbol+" phase=PROTECTION")) {
 684:                     Instant now=Instant.now();
 685:                     // Chronological recent observations preserve stop crossings; never newest-only.
 686:                     var candidates=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND protection_completed_at IS NULL AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY symbol_sequence LIMIT 8",symbol,Timestamp.from(now.minusSeconds(15)),Timestamp.from(now));
 687:                     for(var candidate:candidates) {
 688:                         long id=num(candidate,"source_event_id");String token=deferredProtection.getOrDefault(id,UUID.randomUUID().toString());
 689:                         ProtectionClaim result=claimProtectionAttempt(symbol,id,token);
 690:                         Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_CLAIM_"+result);
 691:                         if(result==ProtectionClaim.BLOCKED||result==ProtectionClaim.REVIEW)break;
 692:                         if(result==ProtectionClaim.CLAIMED) {
 693:                             // Retain only when the locked apply returns before invoking business code.
 694:                             // Any exception/unknown commit removes local continuation authority.
 695:                             boolean finished;
 696:                             try{finished=applyProtection(symbol,id,token);}
 697:                             catch(Exception failure){deferredProtection.remove(id);recordProtectionApplyFailure(symbol,id,token,failure);throw failure;}
 698:                             if(finished){Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_APPLY_RETURNED");deferredProtection.remove(id);}else{Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_APPLY_ORDER_DEFERRED");deferredProtection.put(id,token);break;}
 699:                         } else deferredProtection.remove(id);
 700:                     }
 701:                     // Best-effort terminal bookkeeping, not a correctness-bearing prerequisite.
 702:                     transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{lockSymbol(symbol);jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_outcome='REJECTED' WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND protection_started_at IS NULL AND protection_completed_at IS NULL AND observed_at<? LIMIT 50",symbol,Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(15)));});
 703:                 }catch(Exception failure){log.error("[FIX-142][PROTECTION_DISPATCH_FAILED] symbol={}; started attempts retained",symbol,failure);}
 704:                 finally{activeProtection.remove(symbol);}
 705:             });}catch(RuntimeException failure){activeProtection.remove(symbol);log.error("[FIX-142][PROTECTION_SUBMISSION_FAILED] symbol={}",symbol,failure);}
 706:         }
 707:     }
 708:     boolean applyProtection(String symbol,long id,String token) {
 709:         // FIX-150: this call must occur before opening the apply transaction. An
 710:         // APPLYING attempt from a prior invocation cannot silently enter business again.
 711:         if(!prepareProtectionApply(symbol,id,token))return false;
 712:         // Claim is already committed. Unknown apply/commit leaves its durable start intact.
 713:         return transaction.execute(tx->{
 714:             var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 715:             if(!SharedCutoverPolicy.admitted(state,true)) {restoreUnenteredClaim(id,token);return false;}
 716:             var e=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",id);
 717:             if(!token.equals(e.get("protection_owner")))return true;
 718:             if("REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(e.get("status"))) {restoreUnenteredClaim(id,token);return true;}
 719:             if(e.get("protection_completed_at")!=null)return true;
 720:             if(!"APPLYING".equals(e.get("protection_apply_state")))return false;
 721:             if(earlierProtection(symbol,num(e,"symbol_sequence"),Instant.now())) {
 722:                 // A known pre-business return restores recoverable claim-only evidence.
 723:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_apply_state='CLAIMED' WHERE source_event_id=? AND protection_owner=? AND protection_apply_state='APPLYING'",id,token);
 724:                 return false;
 725:             }
 726:             String reason=protectionReason(symbol,state,e,Instant.now());
 727:             boolean eligible="ELIGIBLE".equals(reason);
 728:             if(eligible){protection.onPrice(symbol,(java.math.BigDecimal)e.get("price"));jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET last_observed_at=?,last_price=? WHERE symbol=?",e.get("observed_at"),e.get("price"),symbol);}
 729:             int completed=jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_outcome=?,eligibility_reason=?,protection_apply_state='COMPLETED' WHERE source_event_id=? AND protection_owner=? AND protection_apply_state='APPLYING' AND protection_completed_at IS NULL",eligible?"APPLIED":"REJECTED",reason,id,token);
 730:             if(completed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-150 apply completion guard changed");
 731:             return true;
 732:         });
 733:     }
 734:     /** Only call from an apply transaction's known pre-business return while its
 735:      * symbol lock is held. Never call after an exception, commit failure or onPrice. */
 736:     private void restoreUnenteredClaim(long id,String token) {
 737:         jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_apply_state='CLAIMED' WHERE source_event_id=? AND protection_owner=? AND protection_apply_state='APPLYING' AND protection_completed_at IS NULL",id,token);
 738:     }
 739:     /** FIX-150: record the initiating failure AFTER the apply transaction unwinds.
 740:      * Recording cannot certify rollback or enable replay; completion wins if the
 741:      * apply actually committed. Keep the first concrete failure through later monitors. */
 742:     private void recordProtectionApplyFailure(String symbol,long id,String token,Exception failure) {
 743:         StringBuilder detail=new StringBuilder("FIX-150 apply failure: ");
 744:         Throwable cause=failure;
 745:         for(int depth=0;cause!=null&&depth<6;depth++,cause=cause.getCause()) {
 746:             if(depth>0)detail.append(" causedBy=");
 747:             detail.append(cause);
 748:         }
 749:         String evidence=detail.substring(0,Math.min(1000,detail.length()));
 750:         try {
 751:             transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
 752:                 lockSymbol(symbol);
 753:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error=CASE WHEN last_error IS NULL OR last_error LIKE 'FIX-142 %' THEN ? ELSE last_error END WHERE source_event_id=? AND protection_owner=? AND protection_completed_at IS NULL",evidence,id,token);
 754:             });
 755:         }catch(Exception recording) {
 756:             log.error("[FIX-150][PROTECTION_FAILURE_RECORD_FAILED] symbol={}, event={}; original error follows",symbol,id,recording);
 757:         }
 758:         log.error("[FIX-150][PROTECTION_APPLY_FAILED] symbol={}, event={}; no automatic effect retry",symbol,id,failure);
 759:     }
 760:     /** Fence and alert aged starts independently of the recent observation window.
 761:      * Timeout is uncertainty, never proof that no effect occurred. Three rows per symbol
 762:      * bounds the normal two-slot inventory plus legacy overflow returned, not rows examined. */
 763:     void monitorProtectionAttempts() {
 764:         if(stopped||!fix142Enabled||!source.enabled())return;
 765:         final List<String> symbols;
 766:         try{symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();}catch(Exception failure){
 767:             // A scheduler must survive inventory/DB failures rather than silently cancel itself.
 768:             Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_MONITOR_FAILED");
 769:             log.error("[FIX-142][PROTECTION_MONITOR_FAILED] symbol inventory unavailable; protection health unknown",failure);
 770:             return;
 771:         }
 772:         for(String symbol:symbols)try {
 773:             // FIX-150 runs even when operational readiness is blocked. Its own durable
 774:             // cutover/position/ownership checks authorize only narrow no-replay retirement.
 775:             if(protectionRecovery!=null) {
 776:                 // FIX-152: completed, never-started REJECTED protection can strand a
 777:                 // phase-1 marker. Resume bookkeeping with a new owner token BEFORE
 778:                 // the separate incomplete-attempt recovery; no nested transaction.
 779:                 protectionRecovery.resumeRejectedMarker(symbol,protocol.processOwner);
 780:                 var recovered=protectionRecovery.recover(symbol);
 781:                 deferredProtection.keySet().removeAll(recovered); // Commit succeeded; drop obsolete local continuation tokens.
 782:                 if(!recovered.isEmpty())log.warn("[FIX-150][EXPIRED_PROTECTION_RETIRED] symbol={}, count={}; no old price/signal replay; inspect atomic audits",symbol,recovered.size());
 783:             }
 784:             var aged=transaction.execute(tx->{
 785:                 lockSymbol(symbol);
 786:                 var rows=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT source_event_id,protection_owner,protection_started_at FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND protection_completed_at IS NULL AND protection_started_at<? ORDER BY protection_started_at,source_event_id LIMIT 3 FOR UPDATE",symbol,Timestamp.from(Instant.now().minusSeconds(30)));
 787:                 for(var row:rows) {
 788:                     // Only fence the started attempt. Never set completed_at or reset its owner.
 789:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='REVIEW_REQUIRED',last_error=COALESCE(last_error,'FIX-142 protection timeout requires audited operator resolution; no automatic retry') WHERE source_event_id=? AND protection_completed_at IS NULL",row.get("source_event_id"));
 790:                 }
 791:                 return rows;
 792:             });
 793:             if(!aged.isEmpty()) {
 794:                 Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_OPERATOR_RECOVERY_REQUIRED");
 795:                 log.error("[FIX-142][PROTECTION_RECOVERY_REQUIRED] symbol={}, attempts={}; live protection may be blocked. Resolve exact attempts with independently verified owner fencing and effect evidence; no automatic release",symbol,aged);
 796:             }
 797:         }catch(Exception failure){
 798:             Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_MONITOR_FAILED");
 799:             log.error("[FIX-142][PROTECTION_MONITOR_FAILED] symbol={}; protection health unknown, inspect immediately",symbol,failure);
 800:         }
 801:     }
 802:     /** Explicitly close an operator-proven protection outcome without replaying onPrice.
 803:      * Evidence text is an authenticated operator attestation, NOT machine liveness proof.
 804:      * Keep status/marker/analysis/observer review independent; marker RESUME remains separate. */
 805:     public void resolveProtection(String symbol,long eventId,String expectedOwner,Instant expectedStartedAt,
 806:             String disposition,String transactionOutcome,String reviewer,String fenceProof,String effectEvidence) {
 807:         if(!fix142Enabled)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 scheduling must be enabled for protection resolution");
 808:         if(symbol==null||symbol.isBlank()||expectedOwner==null||expectedOwner.isBlank()||expectedStartedAt==null)
 809:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Exact symbol, owner and protection start required");
 810:         if(reviewer==null||reviewer.isBlank()||reviewer.length()>160||fenceProof==null||fenceProof.strip().length()<20
 811:                 ||effectEvidence==null||effectEvidence.strip().length()<20)
 812:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Authenticated reviewer, independently established owner fence and detailed effect evidence required");
 813:         final String outcome;
 814:         if("CONFIRMED_APPLIED".equals(disposition)&&"COMMITTED".equals(transactionOutcome))outcome="APPLIED";
 815:         else if("CONFIRMED_NOT_APPLIED".equals(disposition)&&"CONFIRMED_NO_EFFECTS".equals(transactionOutcome))outcome="REJECTED";
 816:         else throw new IllegalArgumentException("Only committed applied effects or independently confirmed no effects may resolve protection; rollback alone is insufficient");
 817:         String proof="startedUtc="+expectedStartedAt+" disposition="+disposition+" transaction="+transactionOutcome+" fenceProof="+fenceProof+" effects="+effectEvidence;
 818:         if(proof.length()>2000)throw new IllegalArgumentException("Resolution evidence exceeds 2000 characters");
 819:         transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
 820:             lockSymbol(symbol); // Same symbol -> delivery lock order as claim/apply/marker resolution.
 821:             var row=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",eventId);
 822:             if(!symbol.equals(row.get("symbol"))||!"1m".equals(row.get("interval_code"))
 823:                     ||!"REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(row.get("status"))||row.get("protection_completed_at")!=null
 824:                     ||!Objects.equals(expectedOwner,row.get("protection_owner"))
 825:                     ||!expectedStartedAt.equals(time(row,"protection_started_at")))
 826:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Protection attempt changed, completed, non-1m or not fenced");
 827:             int changed=jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET protection_completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),protection_outcome=?,protection_apply_state='OPERATOR_RESOLVED' WHERE source_event_id=? AND symbol=? AND interval_code='1m' AND status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' AND protection_owner=? AND protection_started_at=? AND protection_completed_at IS NULL",outcome,eventId,symbol,expectedOwner,Timestamp.from(expectedStartedAt));
 828:             if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("Protection resolution must affect exactly one row");
 829:             int audited=jdbc.update("INSERT INTO shared_delivery_resolution_audit(source_event_id,symbol,operation,old_owner_token,reviewer,evidence) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)",eventId,symbol,"PROTECTION_"+disposition,expectedOwner,reviewer,proof);
 830:             if(audited!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("Protection audit must affect exactly one row");
 831:         });
 832:         deferredProtection.remove(eventId);
 833:         Fix142Metrics.count("PROTECTION_RESOLVED_"+disposition);
 834:         log.warn("[FIX-142][PROTECTION_RESOLVED] symbol={}, event={}, disposition={}, reviewer={}; old observation never replayed; marker/review state unchanged",symbol,eventId,disposition,reviewer);
 835:     }
 836:     /** FIX-142: explicit human-reviewed marker resolution. No PID/age-based release.
 837:      * The authenticated operator supplies independently established old-owner/transaction
 838:      * evidence. Unresolved protection is still refused, regardless of that attestation.
 839:      * Phase-2 observer uncertainty requires a separate explicit disposition. */
 840:     public void resolveMarker(String symbol,long eventId,String expectedOwner,String operation,
 841:             String observerDisposition,String reviewer,String fenceProof,String evidence) {
 842:         if(!fix142Enabled)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-142 scheduling must be enabled for marker resolution");
 843:         if(reviewer==null||reviewer.isBlank()||fenceProof==null||fenceProof.length()<20||evidence==null||evidence.length()<20)
 844:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Independent owner/transaction proof and detailed evidence are required");
 845:         if(!Set.of("RESUME","RELEASE").contains(operation))throw new IllegalArgumentException("Invalid resolution");
 846:         transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
 847:             var state=lockSymbol(symbol);
 848:             if(!"REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(state.get("active_delivery_state"))
 849:                     || !Objects.equals(state.get("active_delivery_event_id"),eventId)
 850:                     || !Objects.equals(state.get("active_delivery_owner_token"),expectedOwner))
 851:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Marker changed or is not fenced");
 852:             var row=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",eventId);
 853:             if(!symbol.equals(row.get("symbol")))throw new IllegalStateException("Marker identity mismatch");
 854:             if(row.get("protection_started_at")!=null&&row.get("protection_completed_at")==null)
 855:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Unresolved protection retains its fence; no release/retry");
 856:             if(row.get("protection_completed_at")!=null&&!Set.of("APPLIED","REJECTED").contains(str(row,"protection_outcome")))
 857:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Unknown legacy protection outcome requires separate evidence repair");
 858:             long phase=num(row,"phase");
 859:             if(operation.equals("RELEASE") && phase!=4)
 860:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Only a durably finished phase stream may release its marker");
 861:             if(operation.equals("RESUME") && phase==4)
 862:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Finished phase stream must release, not resume");
 863:             if(phase==2) {
 864:                 if(!Set.of("CONFIRMED_NOT_INVOKED","RETAIN_REVIEW_SKIP_OBSERVER").contains(observerDisposition))
 865:                     throw new IllegalArgumentException("Phase-2 observer disposition required; never infer absence from rollback");
 866:                 if("RETAIN_REVIEW_SKIP_OBSERVER".equals(observerDisposition))
 867:                     jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET phase=3,observer_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' WHERE source_event_id=?",eventId);
 868:             }
 869:             String newOwner=operation.equals("RESUME")?UUID.randomUUID().toString():null;
 870:             int changed;
 871:             if(operation.equals("RESUME")) {
 872:                 changed=jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET active_delivery_state='ACTIVE',active_delivery_owner_token=?,active_delivery_process_owner=?,active_delivery_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE symbol=? AND active_delivery_event_id=? AND active_delivery_owner_token=? AND active_delivery_state='REVIEW_REQUIRED'",newOwner,protocol.processOwner,symbol,eventId,expectedOwner);
 873:                 // Any delivery error text remains; this is an audited continuation, never a trade replay.
 874:                 jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET status='PROCESSING' WHERE source_event_id=? AND phase<4",eventId);
 875:             } else changed=jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_consumer_state SET active_delivery_state='NONE',active_delivery_event_id=NULL,active_delivery_owner_token=NULL,active_delivery_process_owner=NULL,active_delivery_started_at=NULL,active_delivery_updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE symbol=? AND active_delivery_event_id=? AND active_delivery_owner_token=? AND active_delivery_state='REVIEW_REQUIRED'",symbol,eventId,expectedOwner);
 876:             if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("Marker resolution must affect exactly one row");
 877:             String fullEvidence="oldProcess="+state.get("active_delivery_process_owner")+" fenceProof="+fenceProof+" observer="+observerDisposition+" evidence="+evidence;
 878:             if(fullEvidence.length()>2000)throw new IllegalArgumentException("Resolution evidence exceeds 2000 characters");
 879:             jdbc.update("INSERT INTO shared_delivery_resolution_audit(source_event_id,symbol,operation,old_owner_token,new_owner_token,reviewer,evidence) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",eventId,symbol,operation,expectedOwner,newOwner,reviewer,fullEvidence);
 880:         });
 881:     }
 882:     /** FIX-142 exceptional manual repair: the caller supplies independently verified
 883:      * owner/transaction resolution, and this transaction rechecks all durable evidence.
 884:      * This does NOT relax isolateAnalysisOnlyReview() or retry an old analysis/trade. */
 885:     public void resolveHistoricalAnalysisOnly(long eventId,String expectedOwner,long savedSignal,
 886:             String reviewer,String fenceProof,String evidence) {
 887:         if(reviewer==null||reviewer.isBlank()||fenceProof==null||fenceProof.length()<20||evidence==null||evidence.length()<20)
 888:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Independent owner/transaction resolution and completion evidence required");
 889:         var identity=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT symbol FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=?",eventId);
 890:         String symbol=str(identity,"symbol");
 891:         transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->{
 892:             lockSymbol(symbol);
 893:             var row=jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT * FROM shared_market_event_delivery WHERE source_event_id=? FOR UPDATE",eventId);
 894:             if(num(row,"phase")!=4||!"HISTORICAL_ONLY".equals(row.get("status"))
 895:                 || !"REVIEW_REQUIRED".equals(row.get("analysis_status"))||!Objects.equals(expectedOwner,row.get("owner_token")))
 896:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Only the exact fenced historical analysis-only attempt is eligible");
 897:             if(row.get("protection_started_at")!=null&&row.get("protection_completed_at")==null)
 898:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Unresolved protection is retained");
 899:             if(!jdbc.queryForList("SELECT signal_id FROM signal_processing_work WHERE source_event_id=? OR (symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?) FOR UPDATE",eventId,symbol,row.get("interval_code"),row.get("candle_open_time")).isEmpty())
 900:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Processing work evidence retains review");
 901:             var signals=jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id,symbol,interval_code,candle_open_time FROM trade_signal WHERE id=? FOR UPDATE",savedSignal);
 902:             if(signals.size()!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("Exact persisted signal evidence required");
 903:             for(String key:List.of("symbol","interval_code","candle_open_time"))if(!Objects.equals(row.get(key),signals.getFirst().get(key)))
 904:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Persisted signal identity mismatch");
 905:             if(!jdbc.queryForList("SELECT wt.id FROM wallet_trade wt JOIN trade_signal signal ON signal.id=wt.signal_id WHERE signal.symbol=? AND signal.interval_code=? AND signal.candle_open_time=? FOR UPDATE",symbol,row.get("interval_code"),row.get("candle_open_time")).isEmpty())
 906:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Wallet evidence retains review");
 907:             String proof="signal="+savedSignal+" fenceProof="+fenceProof+" evidence="+evidence;
 908:             if(proof.length()>2000)throw new IllegalArgumentException("Evidence exceeds 2000 characters");
 909:             int changed=jdbc.update("UPDATE shared_market_event_delivery SET analysis_status='ANALYSIS_ONLY_REVIEW' WHERE source_event_id=? AND phase=4 AND status='HISTORICAL_ONLY' AND analysis_status='REVIEW_REQUIRED' AND (owner_token=? OR (owner_token IS NULL AND ? IS NULL))",eventId,expectedOwner,expectedOwner);
 910:             if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("Exceptional repair must affect exactly one row");
 911:             jdbc.update("INSERT INTO shared_delivery_resolution_audit(source_event_id,symbol,operation,old_owner_token,reviewer,evidence) VALUES(?,?,'EXCEPTIONAL_ANALYSIS_ONLY',?,?,?)",eventId,symbol,expectedOwner,reviewer,proof);
 912:         });
 913:     }
 914:     public void dispatchAnalysis() { analysisDispatch.live(); }
 915:     public void dispatchHistoricalAnalysis() { analysisDispatch.historical(); }
 916:     static long num(Map<String,Object> row,String key) { return ((Number)row.get(key)).longValue(); }
 917:     static String str(Map<String,Object> row,String key) { return String.valueOf(row.get(key)); }
 918:     static Instant time(Map<String,Object> row,String key) { return row.get(key)==null?null:((Timestamp)row.get(key)).toInstant(); }
 919:     static boolean truth(Object value) { return Boolean.TRUE.equals(value)||"1".equals(String.valueOf(value)); }
 920:     static String shortError(Exception e) { String s=e.toString();return s.substring(0,Math.min(1000,s.length())); }
 921:     @PreDestroy public void stop() {
 922:         stopped=true;readinessClock.shutdownNow();protectionClock.shutdownNow();freshClock.shutdownNow();protectionPool.shutdown();analysisDispatch.stop();historyClock.shutdown();monitorClock.shutdown();discoveryClock.shutdownNow();priceClock.shutdownNow();analysisClock.shutdown();prices.shutdown();
 923:         long deadline=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(30);
 924:         try {
 925:             boolean priceDone=prices.awaitTermination(30,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
 926:             boolean analysisDone=analysisClock.awaitTermination(Math.max(0,deadline-System.nanoTime()),TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
 927:             boolean historyDone=historyClock.awaitTermination(Math.max(0,deadline-System.nanoTime()),TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
 928:             boolean monitorDone=monitorClock.awaitTermination(Math.max(0,deadline-System.nanoTime()),TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
 929:             boolean readinessDone=readinessClock.awaitTermination(Math.max(0,deadline-System.nanoTime()),TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
 930:             boolean protectionDone=protectionPool.awaitTermination(Math.max(0,deadline-System.nanoTime()),TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
 931:             if(!readinessDone || !protectionDone || !priceDone || !analysisDone || !historyDone || !monitorDone)log.error("[FIX-132][SHUTDOWN_REVIEW_REQUIRED] worker did not drain within 30 seconds; reconcile durable phases before restarting execution");
 932:         } catch(InterruptedException e) {Thread.currentThread().interrupt();log.error("[FIX-132][SHUTDOWN_REVIEW_REQUIRED] drain interrupted",e);}
 933:     }
 934: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/administration/service/DynamicCoinActivationService.java =====
   1: package com.crypto.administration.service;

  17: 
  18:     @Async
  19:     public void activate(String symbol) {
  20:         try {
  21:             webSocketManager.reload();
  22:             log.info("Binance streams reloaded after enabling symbol={}", symbol);
  23:         } catch (Exception exception) {
  24:             log.error("Unable to reload Binance streams after enabling symbol={}", symbol, exception);
  25:         }
  26: 
  27:         try {
  28:             marketDataBootstrapService.bootstrapSymbol(symbol);
  29:             log.info("Dynamic historical bootstrap completed for symbol={}", symbol);
  30:         } catch (Exception exception) {
  31:             log.error("Dynamic historical bootstrap failed for symbol={}", symbol, exception);
  32:         }
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     @Async
  36:     public void reloadStreams() {
  37:         try {
  38:             webSocketManager.reload();
  39:             log.info("Binance streams reloaded after coin configuration change");
  40:         } catch (Exception exception) {
  41:             log.error("Unable to reload Binance streams after coin configuration change", exception);
  42:         }
  43:     }
  44: }
