  92:     private void request(String reason){
  93:         if(!enabled || !"OFF".equals(mode) || !running.compareAndSet(false,true))return;
  94:         try { executor.execute(()-> { try { runPass(reason); } finally { running.set(false); } }); }
  95:         catch(RuntimeException rejected){running.set(false);log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REJECTED] reason={}",reason,rejected);}
  96:     }
  97: 
  98:     private void runPass(String reason){
  99:         if(drain!=null&&!drain.beginReconciliation())return;
 100:         try {
 101:             // The server-time read happens before every REST kline request and is
 102:             // the sole exchange-time reference for this pass.
 103:             Instant exchangeNow=binance.getServerTime();
 104:             Instant databaseNow=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",Timestamp.class).toInstant();
 105:             int[] remaining={Math.max(0,requestBudget)};
 106:             for(Candidate candidate:openCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
 107:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
 108:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
 109:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,false);
 110:             }
 111:             if(remaining[0]>0) for(Candidate candidate:gapCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
 112:                 if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
 113:                 if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
 114:                 recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,true);
 115:             }
 116:             log.debug("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS] reason={}, exchangeNow={}, budget={}",reason,exchangeNow,requestBudget-remaining[0]);
 117:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
 118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS_FAILED] reason={}; no candle was treated as recovered",reason,failure);
 119:         } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeReconciliation();}
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
 123:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
 124:         for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
 125:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
 126:             Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
 127:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
 128:             Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
 129:             result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
 130:                 SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
 131:                 WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
 132:                   AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
 133:                 ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 50
 134:                 """,(rs,n)->{
 135:                     Instant open=rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant();
 136:                     return new Candidate(rs.getString(1),rs.getString(2),open,expectedClose(open,rs.getString(2)));
 137:                 },interval,Timestamp.from(lower),Timestamp.from(latestOpen)));
 138:         }
 139:         return result.stream().filter(c->audit.due(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,databaseNow))
 140:             .filter(this::positiveLossEvidence).toList();
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
 144:     private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
 145:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
 146:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
 147:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
 148:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
 149:             Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
 150:             Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
 151:                 SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
 152:                 """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
 153:             for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
 154:                 if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
 155:                     result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
 156:             }
 157:         }
 158:         return result;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
 162:         var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 163:         int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
 164:         try {
 165:             List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
 166:             List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
 167:             if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
 168:                 List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 169:                 if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
 170:                     terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
 171:                 }
 172:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
 173:             }
 174:             if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
 175:             if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
 176:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
 177:             }
 178:             if(exact.size()!=1) {
 179:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
 180:             }
 181:             BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
 182:             tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 183:                 boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 184:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
 185:                     : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 186:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
 187:                 String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
 188:                 audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
 189:                 if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
 190:             });
 191:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
 195:         long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
 196:         audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
 197:     }
 198:     private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
 199:         // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
 200:         // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
 201:         // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
 202:         // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
 203:         Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
 204:         Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
 205:             candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
 206:         return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
 207:     }
 208:     private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
 209:         tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 210:             audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
 211:             outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
 212:         });
 213:     }
 214:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
 215:     private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
 216:     private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
 217: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalReconciliationAuditRepository.java:1-120 =====
   1: package com.crypto.local;
   2: 
   3: import java.sql.Timestamp;
   4: import java.time.Instant;
   5: import java.util.Optional;
   6: 
   7: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Repository;
   9: 
  10: /** Retry/audit evidence for local reconciliation.  PENDING is the only
  11:  * retryable state; terminal records deliberately never re-enter selection. */
  12: @Repository
  13: public class LocalReconciliationAuditRepository {
  14:     public static final String CANDLE_DATA = "CANDLE_DATA";
  15:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  16: 
  17:     public LocalReconciliationAuditRepository(JdbcTemplate jdbc) { this.jdbc = jdbc; }
  18: 
  19:     public static String candleKey(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  20:         // Canonical fixed precision prevents two textual Instant renderings from
  21:         // creating different audit identities for the same exchange candle.
  22:         return symbol + "|" + interval + "|" + openTime.toEpochMilli();
  23:     }
  24: 
  25:     public Optional<State> findCandle(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  26:         var rows = jdbc.query("""
  27:             SELECT status,attempt_count,next_attempt_at FROM local_reconciliation_audit
  28:             WHERE subject_type=? AND subject_key=?
  29:             """, (rs, n) -> new State(rs.getString(1), rs.getInt(2),
  30:                 rs.getTimestamp(3) == null ? null : rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant()),
  31:             CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime));
  32:         return rows.stream().findFirst();
  33:     }
  34: 
  35:     public boolean due(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, Instant databaseNow) {
  36:         Optional<State> state = findCandle(symbol, interval, openTime);
  37:         return state.isEmpty() || ("PENDING".equals(state.get().status())
  38:             && (state.get().nextAttemptAt() == null || !state.get().nextAttemptAt().isAfter(databaseNow)));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     public void pending(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, int attempts,
  42:                         Instant nextAttempt, String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot) {
  43:         upsert(symbol, interval, openTime, "PENDING", attempts, nextAttempt, reason, exchangeSnapshot, null, null);
  44:     }
  45: 
  46:     public void terminal(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, String status,
  47:                          String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot, Instant binanceOpen, Instant binanceClose) {
  48:         upsert(symbol, interval, openTime, status, 0, null, reason, exchangeSnapshot, binanceOpen, binanceClose);
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     public void markLateLiveClose(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime) {
  52:         jdbc.update("""
  53:             UPDATE local_reconciliation_audit
  54:             SET last_observation='LIVE_CLOSE_AFTER_RECOVERY',
  55:                 late_live_close_at=COALESCE(late_live_close_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
  56:             WHERE subject_type=? AND subject_key=? AND status IN ('RECOVERED','GAP_BACKFILLED')
  57:             """, CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime));
  58:     }
  59: 
  60:     private void upsert(String symbol, String interval, Instant openTime, String status, int attempts,
  61:                         Instant nextAttempt, String reason, Instant exchangeSnapshot,
  62:                         Instant binanceOpen, Instant binanceClose) {
  63:         jdbc.update("""
  64:             INSERT INTO local_reconciliation_audit
  65:             (subject_type,subject_key,symbol,interval_code,candle_open_time,status,failure_reason,attempt_count,
  66:              next_attempt_at,exchange_time_snapshot,binance_open_time,binance_close_time,resolved_at,created_at,updated_at)
  67:             VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
  68:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),failure_reason=VALUES(failure_reason),
  69:              attempt_count=GREATEST(attempt_count,VALUES(attempt_count)),next_attempt_at=VALUES(next_attempt_at),
  70:              exchange_time_snapshot=VALUES(exchange_time_snapshot),binance_open_time=VALUES(binance_open_time),
  71:              binance_close_time=VALUES(binance_close_time),resolved_at=VALUES(resolved_at),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
  72:             """, CANDLE_DATA, candleKey(symbol, interval, openTime), symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(openTime),
  73:             status, reason, attempts, nextAttempt == null ? null : Timestamp.from(nextAttempt),
  74:             exchangeSnapshot == null ? null : Timestamp.from(exchangeSnapshot),
  75:             binanceOpen == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceOpen),
  76:             binanceClose == null ? null : Timestamp.from(binanceClose),
  77:             "PENDING".equals(status) ? null : Timestamp.from(Instant.now()));
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     public record State(String status, int attempts, Instant nextAttemptAt) {}
  81: }

 261: -- Baseline section V4__add_created_at_to_trade_signal.sql
 262: -- Add an immutable audit timestamp to every trade signal.
 263: -- Existing rows are backfilled from generated_at so no historical data is lost.
 264: 
 265: ALTER TABLE trade_signal
 266:     ADD COLUMN created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NULL AFTER generated_at;
 267: 
 268: UPDATE trade_signal
 269: SET created_at = COALESCE(generated_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
 270: WHERE created_at IS NULL;
 271: 
 272: ALTER TABLE trade_signal
 273:     MODIFY COLUMN created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6);
 274: 
 275: CREATE INDEX idx_trade_signal_created_at
 276:     ON trade_signal(created_at);

 608: -- Baseline section V28__create_wallet_portfolio.sql
 609: CREATE TABLE wallet_asset (
 610:     id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
 611:     symbol VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
 612:     quantity DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 613:     average_buy_price_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NULL,
 614:     enabled TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
 615:     created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 616:     updated_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 617:     PRIMARY KEY (id),
 618:     UNIQUE KEY uk_wallet_asset_symbol (symbol)
 619: );
 620: 
 621: CREATE TABLE wallet_settings (
 622:     id BIGINT NOT NULL,
 623:     base_trade_amount_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
 624:     minimum_usdt_reserve DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 625:     automatic_execution_enabled TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 626:     updated_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 627:     PRIMARY KEY (id)
 628: );
 629: 
 630: CREATE TABLE wallet_cash_flow (
 631:     id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
 632:     flow_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
 633:     amount_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 634:     occurred_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 635:     notes VARCHAR(500) NULL,
 636:     created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 637:     PRIMARY KEY (id),
 638:     KEY idx_wallet_cash_flow_time (occurred_at)
 639: );
 640: 
 641: CREATE TABLE wallet_trade (
 642:     id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
 643:     signal_id BIGINT NULL,
 644:     symbol VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
 645:     side VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
 646:     quantity DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 647:     price_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 648:     gross_amount_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 649:     fee_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
 650:     net_amount_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 651:     cost_basis_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NULL,
 652:     realized_pnl_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NULL,
 653:     realized_pnl_percent DECIMAL(20,8) NULL,
 654:     execution_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MANUAL',
 655:     status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EXECUTED',
 656:     executed_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 657:     notes VARCHAR(500) NULL,
 658:     created_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 659:     PRIMARY KEY (id),
 660:     CONSTRAINT fk_wallet_trade_signal FOREIGN KEY (signal_id) REFERENCES trade_signal(id),
 661:     KEY idx_wallet_trade_symbol_time (symbol, executed_at),
 662:     KEY idx_wallet_trade_signal (signal_id)
 663: );
 664: 
 665: CREATE TABLE wallet_snapshot (
 666:     id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
 667:     portfolio_value_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 668:     net_invested_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 669:     total_pnl_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 670:     total_return_percent DECIMAL(20,8) NOT NULL,
 671:     realized_pnl_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 672:     unrealized_pnl_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 673:     available_usdt DECIMAL(30,12) NOT NULL,
 674:     captured_at TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 675:     PRIMARY KEY (id),
 676:     KEY idx_wallet_snapshot_time (captured_at)
 677: );
 678: 
 679: INSERT INTO wallet_asset(symbol, quantity, average_buy_price_usdt, enabled)
 680: VALUES ('USDT', 0, 1, 1)
 681: ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE symbol = VALUES(symbol);
 682: 
 683: INSERT INTO wallet_settings(id, base_trade_amount_usdt, minimum_usdt_reserve, automatic_execution_enabled)
 684: VALUES (1, 100, 0, 0)
 685: ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE id = VALUES(id);

1002: -- Baseline section V48__upgrade_debug_price_move_monitor_to_tracker.sql
1003: -- DEBUG ONLY: Upgrade the V47 threshold/tick monitor into a stateful market move tracker.
1004: -- This table remains isolated from signal generation, execution and wallet logic.
1005: 
1006: ALTER TABLE price_move_monitor_settings
1007:     ADD COLUMN minimum_duration_minutes INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 AFTER window_minutes,
1008:     ADD COLUMN retracement_close_percent DECIMAL(12,6) NOT NULL DEFAULT 30.000000 AFTER minimum_duration_minutes,
1009:     ADD COLUMN cooldown_minutes INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 10 AFTER retracement_close_percent;
1010: 
1011: UPDATE price_move_monitor_settings
1012: SET minimum_duration_minutes = 1,
1013:     retracement_close_percent = 30.000000,
1014:     cooldown_minutes = 10
1015: WHERE id = 1;
1016: 
1017: -- V47 rows were threshold-tick events and are intentionally cleared so the
1018: -- new week starts with only one-row-per-completed-move tracker semantics.
1019: DELETE FROM price_move_event;
1020: 
1021: 
1022: -- Baseline section V49__filter_debug_tracker_to_medium_high_moves.sql
1023: ALTER TABLE price_move_event
1024:     ADD COLUMN importance_level VARCHAR(10) NULL AFTER duration_seconds;
1025: 
1026: UPDATE price_move_event
1027: SET importance_level = CASE
1028:     WHEN ABS(change_percent) >= 1.500000 THEN 'HIGH'
1029:     WHEN ABS(change_percent) >= 0.750000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
1030:     ELSE 'LOW'
1031: END;
1032: 
1033: DELETE FROM price_move_event
1034: WHERE duration_seconds <= 300
1035:    OR ABS(change_percent) < 0.750000;
1036: 
1037: ALTER TABLE price_move_event
1038:     MODIFY COLUMN importance_level VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL;
1039: 
1040: UPDATE price_move_monitor_settings
1041: SET minimum_duration_minutes = GREATEST(minimum_duration_minutes, 6)
1042: WHERE id = 1;

1514: -- Baseline section V69__create_trade_signal_test_parity.sql

1517: CREATE TABLE trade_signal_test LIKE trade_signal;

1519: -- candle to be regenerated in multiple independent test runs, so run-scoping replaces

1528:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_run_time (test_run_id, generated_at),
1529:     ADD INDEX idx_trade_signal_test_run_interval (test_run_id, interval_code);
1530:

1865: -- Baseline section V82__fix_118_profit_lock_tp_extension_rebase.sql
1866: -- FIX-118 behavioral phase: preserve earned Profit Lock history across TP extension
1867: -- without letting protection calculated against the superseded TP remain executable.
1868: ALTER TABLE wallet_managed_position
1869:     ADD COLUMN profit_lock_state VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INACTIVE' AFTER profit_lock_activated_at,
1870:     ADD COLUMN profit_lock_rebase_started_at TIMESTAMP(6) NULL AFTER profit_lock_state;
1871: 
1872: -- Mandatory legacy backfill: every position that already has an earned lock remains ACTIVE.
1873: UPDATE wallet_managed_position
1874: SET profit_lock_state = 'ACTIVE'
1875: WHERE profit_lock_active = 1;
1876: 
1877: -- Replay carries the same persisted lifecycle state. Archive remains schema-identical because
1878: -- regression archival uses INSERT ... SELECT * semantics.
1879: ALTER TABLE wallet_position_test
1880:     ADD COLUMN profit_lock_state VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INACTIVE' AFTER profit_lock_price_usdt,
1881:     ADD COLUMN profit_lock_rebase_started_at TIMESTAMP(6) NULL AFTER profit_lock_state;
1882: UPDATE wallet_position_test
1883: SET profit_lock_state = 'ACTIVE'
1884: WHERE profit_lock_active = 1;
1885: 
1886: ALTER TABLE wallet_position_test_archive
1887:     ADD COLUMN profit_lock_state VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'INACTIVE' AFTER profit_lock_price_usdt,
1888:     ADD COLUMN profit_lock_rebase_started_at TIMESTAMP(6) NULL AFTER profit_lock_state;
1889: UPDATE wallet_position_test_archive
1890: SET profit_lock_state = 'ACTIVE'
1891: WHERE profit_lock_active = 1;

2143: -- Baseline section V98__fix_141_reconciliation_scan_index.sql
2144: -- FIX-141 Phase 2 review correction: SharedAnalysisDispatcher#reconcileAnalysisOnlyReviews and
2145: -- #reconcileOwnerTimeoutReviews scan shared_market_event_delivery GLOBALLY (not per-symbol like
2146: -- claim()), filtering by analysis_status and paging via a source_event_id cursor in
2147: -- "ORDER BY source_event_id" order. Neither existing FIX-138 index supports that access path:
2148: -- idx_fix138_analysis_close is led by symbol (no symbol filter in these global scans), and
2149: -- idx_fix138_analysis_timeout is led by analysis_status but then analysis_started_at, so rows
2150: -- are grouped by analysis_started_at before source_event_id - not sorted by source_event_id
2151: -- across the whole analysis_status slice, which "ORDER BY source_event_id" would otherwise need
2152: -- to satisfy with a filesort over the entire REVIEW_REQUIRED backlog on every scheduled pass.
2153: --
2154: -- This index leads with analysis_status (the one true equality filter both scans share) and
2155: -- sorts by source_event_id, so the two reconciliation queries can read analysis_status=
2156: -- 'REVIEW_REQUIRED' rows already in source_event_id order starting from the cursor, instead of
2157: -- sorting the whole matching set with a filesort.
2158: --
2159: -- What this index does NOT cover: analysis_completed_at, phase and analysis_started_at are not
2160: -- part of it, so those predicates are not answered from the index alone - each candidate row
2161: -- the index yields still needs a row lookup to evaluate them. LIMIT 64 (or 16) bounds how many
2162: -- ROWS ARE RETURNED after those predicates are applied, not how many rows are READ to find them:
2163: -- if REVIEW_REQUIRED rows are numerous and the remaining predicates are selective, this can still
2164: -- examine far more than 64/16 rows per pass. Whether that is acceptable, and whether MySQL
2165: -- actually avoids a filesort with this index in place, has NOT been verified against a deployed
2166: -- MySQL instance - EXPLAIN against real data and realistic REVIEW_REQUIRED volume must happen
2167: -- before enabling scheduled reconciliation in production.
2168: CREATE INDEX idx_fix141_reconcile_scan
2169:  ON shared_market_event_delivery(analysis_status, source_event_id);
2170:

docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:1:# Replay / Production Parity Contract
docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:5:- MySQL/Binance timestamps are UTC. Backend and Replay interpret them as UTC.
docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:7:- Fresh Replay signals are generated through the same TechnicalIndicatorService + AnalysisService decision path as Production.
docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:10:- Replay consumes those exact price observations before same-time candle-close signals, matching Production event ordering.
docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:13:- A replay window without persisted `market_price_event` rows is degraded historical replay and must not be reported as exact Production parity.
docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:17:Replay never writes to the real wallet or Production position tables. Wallet/position persistence remains simulated by design so a test cannot move real money. This is side-effect isolation, not separate trading logic.
docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:21:After V64 is deployed, allow Production to collect live price events. Replay the identical UTC interval and compare BUY signal/decision, allocation, entry price, TP/SL/profit-lock events, SELL reason, exit price and exit time. UI reports should convert timestamps to the user's local timezone only at presentation time.
docs\REPLAY_PRODUCTION_PARITY.md:24:Exact Replay now requires the same canonical price authority used by Production at BUY execution time. Replay advances `ExecutionReplayScope` with persisted UTC `market_price_event` observations before evaluating a signal. Both paths then run `revalidateAtExecutionPrice`, size from that price, and persist/use that price as the fill. The historical `TradeSignal.latestPrice` remains immutable decision evidence and is never rewritten to imitate the fill.

codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:1:# Local candle coverage check — 2026-10-09
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:12:- Cause confirmed from the WebSocket lifecycle log: generation 1 was revoked at `2026-10-09T04:35:00.133951100Z`; generation 2 was admitted at `04:35:01.369103400Z` (1,236 ms interruption). The affected 1m and 5m candles had both closed at the reconnect boundary. Binance did not replay their final closed-kline events, leaving the already-created local rows open rather than finalized.
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:29:- Primary cause: the local candle-quality interruption stopped 1m ETH signals after 07:34 KSA. There were no ETH 1m signals from 07:34 to 10:26 KSA, the period containing most of the rise. The interrupted 1m and 5m rows were repaired at about 10:28 KSA, so analysis resumed only near the later consolidation/breakout attempt. This is a confirmed data-availability gap, not proof that the strategy would have bought near 07:54 KSA.
codex-docs\INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:31:- Assessment: the trade followed the implemented rules once local analysis resumed, and the trade was entered/exited promptly. It was nevertheless a late entry relative to the full 07:54–10:33 KSA advance because the system lacked signal coverage during the main move. The data gap is an operational defect already covered by the local-candle recovery deployment gate. Whether the unobserved earlier period would have produced an executable BUY is uncertain without a controlled Replay run; none was started because that would create replay state.

 463: 
 464:     /**
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 466:      * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
 467:      * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
 468:      */
 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }
 490: 
 491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
 492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
 493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
 494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
 495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
 496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
 497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
 498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
 499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
 500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
 501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
 502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
 503:             }
 504:         }
 505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
 506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
 507:     }
 508: 
 509:     private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 510:         return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 511:                 symbol, interval, start, end);
 512:     }
 513: 
 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }

 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,

 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));

 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));

 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals

 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {

 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET

1: # Local candle coverage check - 2026-10-09
29: - Primary cause: the local candle-quality interruption stopped 1m ETH signals after 07:34 KSA. There were no ETH 1m signals from 07:34 to 10:26 KSA, the period containing most of the rise. The interrupted 1m and 5m rows were repaired at about 10:28 KSA, so analysis resumed only near the later consolidation/breakout attempt. This is a confirmed data-availability gap, not proof that the strategy would have bought near 07:54 KSA.
31: - Assessment: the trade followed the implemented rules once local analysis resumed, and the trade was entered/exited promptly. It was nevertheless a late entry relative to the full 07:54-10:33 KSA advance because the system lacked signal coverage during the main move. The data gap is an operational defect already covered by the local-candle recovery deployment gate. Whether the unobserved earlier period would have produced an executable BUY is uncertain without a controlled Replay run; none was started because that would create replay state.

  32:     @Override public void migrate(Context context) throws Exception {
  33:         Connection connection = context.getConnection();
  34:         if (!"MySQL".equals(connection.getMetaData().getDatabaseProductName())
  35:                 || connection.getMetaData().getDatabaseMajorVersion() < 8) {
  36:             throw new SQLException("FIX-149 requires MySQL 8 with descending-index support");
  37:         }
  38:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement(); ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery("SELECT DATABASE()")) {
  39:             if (!result.next() || !"crypto_ai".equals(result.getString(1))) {
  40:                 throw new SQLException("FIX-149 Flyway history must run in crypto_ai; index target is explicitly crypto_ai_v2");
  41:             }
  42:         }
  43:         try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
  44:                 "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema=? AND table_name=? AND engine='InnoDB'")) {
  45:             statement.setString(1, SOURCE_SCHEMA);
  46:             statement.setString(2, TABLE);
  47:             try (ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery()) {
  48:                 if (!result.next() || result.getInt(1) != 1) {
  49:                     throw new SQLException("FIX-149 requires existing crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event on the trader MySQL server InnoDB table; no empty replacement is created");
  50:                 }
  51:             }
  52:         }
  53:         List<String> additions = new ArrayList<>();
  54:         if (!existingIndexMatches(connection, OBSERVED, OBSERVED_PARTS)) {
  55:             additions.add("ADD INDEX " + OBSERVED + " (symbol,interval_code,source,classification,observed_at,symbol_sequence)");
  56:         }

  59:         }
  60:         if (additions.isEmpty()) {
  61:             LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEXES_ALREADY_PRESENT] schema=crypto_ai_v2 historySchema=crypto_ai utc=" + Instant.now());
  62:             return;
  63:         }
  64:         long previousLockWait;
  65:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement(); ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery("SELECT @@SESSION.lock_wait_timeout")) {
  66:             if (!result.next()) throw new SQLException("FIX-149 cannot read metadata-lock timeout");
  67:             previousLockWait = result.getLong(1);
  68:         }
  69:         Instant start = Instant.now();
  70:         long startNanos = System.nanoTime();
  71:         LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_START] schema=crypto_ai_v2 historySchema=crypto_ai table=" + TABLE + " startUtc=" + start);
  72:         // One ALTER builds both missing indexes. Ten seconds bounds metadata-lock wait,
  73:         // not the build duration, resource use, or every possible InnoDB wait.
  74:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {
  75:             statement.execute("SET SESSION lock_wait_timeout=10");
  76:             try {
  77:                 statement.execute("ALTER TABLE `" + SOURCE_SCHEMA + "`.`" + TABLE + "`" + " " + String.join(", ", additions) + ", ALGORITHM=INPLACE, LOCK=NONE");
  78:                 LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_END] startUtc=" + start + " endUtc=" + Instant.now()
  79:                     + " durationMs=" + ((System.nanoTime() - startNanos) / 1_000_000) + " outcome=SUCCESS");
  80:             } catch (SQLException failure) {
  81:                 LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.ERROR, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_END] startUtc=" + start + " endUtc=" + Instant.now()
  82:                     + " durationMs=" + ((System.nanoTime() - startNanos) / 1_000_000) + " outcome=FAILED sqlState=" + failure.getSQLState());
  83:                 throw failure;
  84:             } finally {
  85:                 statement.execute("SET SESSION lock_wait_timeout=" + previousLockWait);
  86:             }
  87:         }
  88:     }
  89: 
  90:     static boolean existingIndexMatches(Connection connection, String name, List<String> expected) throws SQLException {
  91:         List<String> actual = new ArrayList<>();
  92:         try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
  93:                 "SELECT column_name,collation,sub_part,non_unique,index_type,is_visible FROM information_schema.statistics "
  94:                 + "WHERE table_schema=? AND table_name=? AND index_name=? ORDER BY seq_in_index")) {
  95:             statement.setString(1, SOURCE_SCHEMA);
  96:             statement.setString(2, TABLE);
  97:             statement.setString(3, name);
  98:             try (ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery()) {
  99:                 while (result.next()) {
 100:                     if (result.getObject("sub_part") != null || result.getInt("non_unique") != 1
 101:                             || !"BTREE".equals(result.getString("index_type")) || !"YES".equals(result.getString("is_visible"))) {
 102:                         throw new SQLException("FIX-149 incompatible existing index " + name + "; inspect before deployment");
 103:                     }
 104:                     actual.add(result.getString("column_name") + ":" + result.getString("collation"));
 105:                 }
 106:             }
 107:         }
 108:         if (actual.isEmpty()) return false;
 109:         if (!actual.equals(expected)) throw new SQLException("FIX-149 incompatible existing index " + name + "; expected " + expected + " found " + actual);
 110:         return true;
 111:     }
 112: }

===== codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:85-140 =====
  85: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` is an existing Java Flyway migration
  86: that requires the collector event table for a fresh installation. A trader-only
  87: bootstrap policy is therefore **pending** and remains a release blocker. Do
  88: not edit applied Flyway migrations or Flyway history.
  89: 
  90: ## Implemented, unverified: local-candle architecture (FIX-153)
  91: 
  92: The implemented intended mode is `shared-market.mode=OFF` with trader-owned Binance kline
  93: ingestion and local candle writes. The shared executors and direct-ingestion
  94: path must never run together.
  95: 
  96: Implemented safeguards:
  97: 
  98: - Candle writes are monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or overwrite a
  99:   closed candle.
 100: - A closed-candle transition is admitted exactly once using an atomic upsert
 101:   result plus in-process admission coordination.
 102: - A bounded local detector recovers a closed candle that was committed before
 103:   the in-memory event/dispatcher could start analysis.
 104: - Fresh candles may execute; stale candles are analysed historically only, or
 105:   their already-owned work is expired without business execution.
 106: - Analysis lanes are bounded. Under overload, 1m may retain only the latest
 107:   pending candle; higher intervals use an explicit bounded FIFO policy.
 108: - Historic shared `REVIEW_REQUIRED` evidence must not block OFF-mode work.
 109: - OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must use local-candle health and must not
 110:   query retired shared-market tables.
 111: - Current REST backfill can finalise an interrupted open row but cannot mint a
 112:   runtime close token or dispatch analysis. Runtime close tokens plus
 113:   provenance keep backfill out of detector admission. The approved, pending
 114:   automatic-reconciliation extension will also insert an exact wholly absent
 115:   candle only inside the active quality window, with `BACKFILL` provenance and
 116:   the same no-token/no-dispatch boundary.
 117: - Observed local behavior (2026-10-09): a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close
 118:   boundary can leave the already-persisted 1m/5m rows open because Binance does
 119:   not replay the final closed-kline event. The current detector recovers missing
 120:   analysis admission, not missing close facts. An authoritative, bounded REST
 121:   repair may therefore finalise the exact `closed=0` row or, under the approved
 122:   gap-backfill scope, insert an exact absent identity. It must preserve existing
 123:   provenance, use `BACKFILL` for an insertion, create no close-transition token,
 124:   and never dispatch historical work. Automatic reconciliation of this reconnect
 125:   case remains a FIX-153 release gate; the one-time local repair is evidence,
 126:   not proof that the gap is closed.
 127: - Approved pending reconciliation design: gap backfill is insert-only (no
 128:   duplicate-key update); it can never alter a live row that appears during a
 129:   REST request. Candidate selection is bounded to the active quality window;
 130:   terminal audit records are not retried. Every candle/work route must produce
 131:   a durable terminal outcome or remain explicitly retryable after a transient
 132:   failure. Historical-only processing has no execution authority.
 133: - The pending universal mutation barrier is an in-memory drain participant,
 134:   not a database or persistent trading fence. Outside drain it must not delay,
 135:   queue, or block fresh trading, signals, or protection. Open-position
 136:   protection during application replacement remains an unresolved release gate.
 137: - Local source now contains the first reconciliation implementation slice:
 138:   V107 durable audit/outcome tables, update-only authoritative open-row repair,
 139:   pure insert-only gap backfill, a dedicated recovery executor, a reconnect
 140:   request hook, and drain participation. It is disabled by default and remains

===== claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:35-65 =====
  35: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` (Java migration) adds indexes to
  36: `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`; a fresh trader-only install fails
  37: without that schema. Release blocker; do not edit applied migrations.
  38: 
  39: ## Ingestion modes (`shared-market.mode`, default `OFF`)
  40: 
  41: - **OFF (FIX-153)** ƒ?" trader connects to Binance and writes `crypto_ai.candle`.
  42:   `LocalTraderInstance` requires an absolute `local-candle.instance-lock-path`
  43:   (env `LOCAL_TRADER_INSTANCE_LOCK`) and holds an OS file lock: one trader
  44:   process per host. Empty value fails OFF-mode startup.
  45: - **LIVE / OBSERVE (legacy/shared)** ƒ?" `SharedMarketConsumer` polls collector
  46:   stream tables into local `shared_market_event_delivery`;
  47:   `SharedAnalysisDispatcher` drives analysis. Direct ingestion entry points
  48:   (`BinanceWebSocketManager`, `BinanceKlineService.processKline`,
  49:   `MarketDataService.importCandles`, `MarketDataBootstrapService`) are
  50:   disabled in LIVE. The two paths must never run together.
  51: 
  52: ## Primary flow (OFF mode)
  53: 
  54: ```text
  55: Binance WS (combined stream, enabled coins x 1m/5m/1h)
  56:  -> BinanceWebSocketManager (owner thread "fix131-ws-owner", generation gate)
  57:  -> BinanceWebSocketHandler.handleTextMessage (socket thread, synchronous)
  58:  -> BinanceKlineService.processKline        (@Transactional NEVER, drain check)
  59:  -> KlineTransactionCoordinator.process     (each phase REQUIRES_NEW, READ_COMMITTED, 30s)
  60:      1 INPUT        CandleRepository.upsertRuntime + MarketPriceEventService.recordPending (1m)
  61:      2 PROTECTION   LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice (1m; one retry on initial-lock deadlock)
  62:      3 OBSERVER     PriceMoveMonitorService.onPrice (errors swallowed)
  63:      4 CLOSE        publish CandleClosedEvent only if this receipt won the 0->1 close token
  64:  -> CandleClosedEventListener (AFTER_COMMIT, not @Async)
  65:  -> CandleAnalysisDispatcher (per symbol|interval lane)

===== claude-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md:25-50 =====
  25:    `CryptoAccountConfigurationService` limits (max order, exposure, loss,
  26:    slippage) are not read by the paper entry path.
  27: 5. **Single-threaded ingestion and protection.** Candle upserts, price events
  28:    and `LivePositionProtectionService` run synchronously on the WebSocket
  29:    socket thread; slow protection delays all later kline messages.
  30: 6. **`websocket.reconnect-delay-seconds` is display-only.** Reconnect backoff
  31:    uses `health-check-seconds`; the property is only returned by
  32:    `BinanceStatusController`.
  33: 7. **Wallet exit failure does not block paper close.** In
  34:    `PaperTradingService.completeClose` a wallet exit exception is logged and
  35:    the paper position still closes ƒ?" a potential paper/wallet divergence to
  36:    check in investigations.
  37: 8. **Fresh trader-only install blocked by V103** (collector schema
  38:    dependency); known release blocker.
  39: 9. **FIX-153 work is uncommitted** in the working tree (including V99-V106,
  40:    B105 and `com/crypto/local`).
  41: 
  42: Items 2-7 are observations, not confirmed defects; whether each is intended
  43: has not been confirmed with the user.

## FINDINGS

- The checked-in default is `shared-market.mode=OFF`. In OFF, the trader itself owns Binance kline WebSocket ingestion, REST bootstrap/import, local persistence, runtime close dispatch, and optional reconciliation. Candles are written to the local `crypto_ai.candle` table.

- `LIVE` is the retained collector architecture: direct WebSocket, bootstrap, and import are disabled; the trader reads collector tables from `crypto_ai_v2` and consumes them through local delivery tables.

- `OBSERVE` is architecturally contradictory to the pending direction. Direct trader ingestion remains enabled because only `LIVE` disables it, while `SharedMarketConsumer` also schedules collector discovery, price, and analysis loops. It does not execute collector-driven protection/analysis—it marks deliveries `OBSERVED`—but it still connects to and reads collector infrastructure alongside direct ingestion. This contradicts the stated “do not run direct ingestion and shared-market executors together” rule.

- Runtime close handling is structurally sound only for the WebSocket path: `upsertRuntime` writes a close-transition token only when an open row becomes closed; only the receipt owning that token publishes the close event. The event is after commit and analysis is submitted to an executor rather than run on the Binance thread.

- The ordinary REST import path is materially unsafe versus the pending design. `MarketDataService.importCandles()` sends already-final Binance candles to `CandleRepository.upsert()`, whose `BACKFILL` duplicate-key clause can update an existing `closed=0` runtime row to closed, without a token, outcome, or dispatch. This can happen in the reachable overlap where dynamic activation reloads WebSockets before bootstrap, and through the manual import API.

- That unsafe ordinary backfill can leave a closed candle permanently unanalysed: a later WebSocket close cannot own a token; the local detector selects only token-bearing non-`BACKFILL` rows; reconciliation selects only `closed=0` rows. No terminal `candle_analysis_outcome` is written by that path. The repository comment claiming backfill “inserts missing identities only” is false for the implemented `upsert()` method.

- A separate reconciliation implementation does meet several pending-design mechanics: it is OFF-only; exact recovery only finalizes open non-backfill rows; gap repair is insert-if-absent; both avoid tokens and record historical-only outcomes. However, it is disabled in checked-in config, so automatic reconnect recovery is not part of the checked-in operational behavior.

- Reconciliation also does not meet the durable-age-out requirement. `PENDING` audit rows are due indefinitely with a maximum retry delay but no attempt or time ceiling. Once candidates age out of the active quality window, they are no longer selected and can remain `PENDING` without a terminal analysis outcome. The open-candidate query applies `LIMIT 50` before due/loss-evidence filtering, allowing ineligible old rows to crowd out eligible work.

- No reviewed implementation establishes shared Binance REST handling for 429/418, `Retry-After`, rate budgets, or bans across bootstrap, reconciliation, and exchange-clock callers. The REST client creates separate clients and timeouts, but no visible shared rate-limit policy.

- Fresh Replay loads every closed candle solely by closed/time predicates and then generates fresh signals for every candle in its timeline. It does not filter `ingestion_source`, close token, reconciliation audit, or local analysis outcome. Therefore source review does not prove parity with production’s deliberate exclusion of recovered/backfilled/token-less candles from live close dispatch.

- The target direction remains not deployment-ready. The source has substantial local-candle implementation, but the checked-in mode is not evidence of a production deployment. No production systems were accessed.

## EVIDENCE

- **Default OFF and configuration contradiction:** `application.yml:40-50` sets reconciliation disabled; `application.yml:420-432` sets `shared-market.mode` default `OFF`; `application.yml:447-459` begins a second top-level `local-candle` block. Duplicate top-level YAML keys require runtime parser validation; source review cannot determine whether startup rejects or overrides one mapping.

- **Trader WebSocket ownership outside LIVE:** `BinanceWebSocketManager.start()` returns only when the mode is LIVE: `src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java:95-103`. `BinanceKlineService` likewise treats only LIVE as shared: `src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:23,55-135`.

- **Trader REST/bootstrap ownership outside LIVE:** `MarketDataService.importCandles()` rejects only LIVE and calls Binance server-time/klines before local write: `src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataService.java:19-55`. Bootstrap runs unless LIVE: `src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataBootstrapService.java:48-69`.

- **Local versus collector table ownership:** OFF uses the local JDBC reader/writer; non-OFF creates a collector pool and validates its schema: `src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:19-55`. Candle reads route through that selected reader: `src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:32-36`. LIVE collector event reads include `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event` and cursor tables: `src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:330,345`.

- **Tokened close transition and async dispatch:** `CandleRepository.upsertRuntime()` reads back the exact transition token: `CandleRepository.java:107-124`; WebSocket processing publishes only when it owns it: `BinanceKlineService.java:101-121`; transaction order is input → protection → observer → close dispatch: `KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:36-92`; the event listener is `AFTER_COMMIT`: `CandleClosedEventListener.java:30-33`; dispatcher uses its executor: `CandleAnalysisDispatcher.java:102-123`.

- **Unsafe ordinary backfill:** `MarketDataService` imports all exchange-final rows as `closed=true`: `MarketDataService.java:34-50`. `CandleRepository.upsert()` assigns `BACKFILL`: `CandleRepository.java:99-105`. Its duplicate clause updates open rows and then applies `closed=GREATEST(...)`, without token assignment: `CandleRepository.java:173-188`. Dynamic activation reloads WebSockets before bootstrap: `DynamicCoinActivationService.java:18-32`. The public import endpoint calls this same method: `MarketDataController.java:16-23`.

- **Why no recovery/admission follows:** detector requires both `closed=1` and a token, excluding `BACKFILL`: `LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:34-50`; reconciliation only finds `closed=0`: `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:122-140`.

- **Implemented recovery mechanics, disabled operationally:** `LocalCandleReconciliationService` runs only if enabled and OFF: `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:92-96`; it calls update-only recovery or insert-only gap repair and records `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE`: `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:161-191`. Repository methods enforce those distinct policies: `CandleRepository.java:126-167`.

- **Indefinite audit pending:** audit due logic has no terminal deadline: `LocalReconciliationAuditRepository.java:35-43`; retry delay caps at 300 seconds without attempt limit: `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:194-196`; active-window selection excludes older rows: `LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:125-140,143-158`.

- **OBSERVE overlap:** WebSocket/direct ingestion is disabled only for LIVE above. Meanwhile shared loops run whenever mode is not OFF: `SharedMarketConsumer.java:136-151`; OBSERVE reads collector cursors and events: `SharedMarketConsumer.java:322-379`; it marks each local delivery `OBSERVED` before protection/analysis: `SharedMarketConsumer.java:477-481`.

- **Replay mismatch:** Replay dataset queries every `closed=1` candle: `RegressionTestWorker.java:469-506`; the fresh path explicitly processes “EVERY closed candle”: `RegressionTestWorker.java:575-584`.

- **Bootstrap blocker:** Java migration V103 explicitly requires `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`: `src/main/java/db/migration/V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes.java:14-50`. B105 claims a trader-only baseline, but this review did not run Flyway/MySQL to prove that baseline migration semantics work: `src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:1-4`; dependencies are Spring Boot 4.1.0/Flyway starter/MySQL module: `pom.xml:7-9,56-65`.

## OBJECTIONS

1. Ordinary REST import violates the pending pure-insert gap-backfill rule and can consume a live close without durable analysis admission or terminal outcome.

2. Automatic recovery is disabled by default and reconciliation can leave PENDING records without a terminal outcome after they leave the quality window.

3. The required shared Binance rate-limit/ban control is unproven and not present in reviewed REST client paths.

4. OBSERVE permits direct ingestion plus shared-market executors and collector connectivity, contrary to AGENTS.md’s target separation. It is not double trading execution, but it is still an architectural breach.

5. Replay parity is not demonstrated because replay input selection differs from the production local close-admission boundary.

6. Fresh trader-only bootstrap remains unverified because V103 requires collector infrastructure and B105 has not been validated.

7. The duplicate `local-candle` YAML key may make the effective reconciliation settings ambiguous or reject startup.

## UNKNOWNS

- Effective production mode, environment overrides, artifact, database schema, Flyway history, and deployment status. Production was not accessed.
- YAML duplicate-key behavior for the exact Spring configuration stack.
- Flyway baseline behavior and successful MySQL 8 fresh install using B105.
- Full build/test, crash/restart, race, Java 21, and MySQL validation results; none were executed.
- Whether another unreviewed service or infrastructure layer supplies Binance-wide rate-limit control.
- Open-position protection across application replacement remains an unresolved release gate.

## VERDICT

REJECTED
